 /**
  * Diagnostics only (perf homework 2026-09-20, DATABASE_MODE_HOMEWORK §4ac):
diff --git a/core/src/main/java/com/legend/diagnostics/VerdictCensus.java b/core/src/main/java/com/legend/diagnostics/VerdictCensus.java
new file mode 100644

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.diagnostics;
+
+/**
+ * THE SQL VERDICT CENSUS (leg 3.0, docs/DATABASE_MODE_HOMEWORK_2026_09_18.md §4): per assert family, what was
+ * adjudicated, judged in the database, routed elsewhere, unjudged or raised -- attribution only, no verdict reads it.
+ * Counted by the asserts' planning and by their run alike, so it lives below both (the plan-as-data design's step 10,
+ * 2026-11-08: moved out of the judges' {@code CanonicalDivergence}; its never-set mute switch left behind).
+ */
+public final class VerdictCensus {
+
+    private VerdictCensus() {
+    }
+
+    // ── leg 3.0 (docs/DATABASE_MODE_HOMEWORK_2026_09_18.md §4): the
+    // CLAIM / DECLINE census per assert family — what the SQL canon
+    // already judges and what it declines, by reason. Attribution
+    // only: no verdict reads any of this. CURRENT_FAMILY is the assert
+    // family AssertVerdicts is adjudicating (set at its one entry), so
+    // a decline recorded deeper (TdsCompare, the wrap) lands on it.
+    private static final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<String>
+            CURRENT_FAMILY = new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicReference<>("?");
+    private static final java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<String, Long>
+            SQL_CENSUS = new java.util.concurrent.ConcurrentHashMap<>();
+
+
+    /** An assert of {@code family} was judged by a route that has NO
+     * byte channel (the SQL-text rows arm, the rendered-text arm, the
+     * grid-pair arm): counted so "never attempted" has a reason. */
+    public static void sqlRoute(String family, String route) {
+        SQL_CENSUS.merge("not-attempted " + family + " " + route, 1L, Long::sum);
+        CHANNEL_SEEN.set(true);
+    }
+
+    /** The assert being adjudicated RAISED before any channel judged
+     * (a side that errors, a failed verdict thrown as AssertFailed):
+     * counted on the current family so "never attempted" has a reason. */
+    public static void sqlRaised() {
+        if (CHANNEL_SEEN.get()) {
+            return;   // a judged assert that then failed is not "never attempted"
+        }
+        SQL_CENSUS.merge("not-attempted " + CURRENT_FAMILY.get() + " raised",
+                1L, Long::sum);
+        CHANNEL_SEEN.set(true);   // a nested entry's raise is one raise
+    }
+
+    /** The current assert's switch arm yielded null (not this arm's
+     * shape; the generic path continues): counted, so "adjudicated"
+     * minus the channels is explained. */
+    public static void sqlFellThrough() {
+        SQL_CENSUS.merge("not-attempted " + CURRENT_FAMILY.get() + " fell-through",
+                1L, Long::sum);
+    }
+
+    /** Whether any channel (claim, decline, route) spoke for the current
+     * assert — set by them, reset at {@link #sqlFamily}. */
+    private static final java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean CHANNEL_SEEN =
+            new java.util.concurrent.atomic.AtomicBoolean();
+
+    /** A statement-root assert enters the adjudicator, BEFORE its family
+     * is known (the lineage, quantified, if-branch and SQL-text root
+     * arms run first): a raise from there lands on its own row. */
+    public static void sqlEnter() {
+        CURRENT_FAMILY.set("(pre-arm)");
+        CHANNEL_SEEN.set(false);
+    }
+
+    /** Leg 3.1: database mode decided the assert (the verdict row). */
+    public static void sqlJudgedInDatabase(String family) {
+        SQL_CENSUS.merge("judged-in-database " + family, 1L, Long::sum);
+        CHANNEL_SEEN.set(true);
+    }
+
+    /** Leg 3.1: database mode could NOT decide the assert — the assert
+     * failed with this reason; the per-mode ceiling the register pins. */
+    public static void sqlUnjudged(String family, String reason) {
+        com.legend.diagnostics.Diagnostics.judgeDecline("unjudged", family, reason);
+        int c = reason.indexOf(':');
+        String head = c < 0 ? reason : reason.substring(0, c);
+        SQL_CENSUS.merge("unjudged " + family + " " + head, 1L, Long::sum);
+        CHANNEL_SEEN.set(true);
+    }
+
+    /** An assert of {@code family} enters adjudication. */
+    public static void sqlFamily(String family) {
+        CURRENT_FAMILY.set(family);
+        CHANNEL_SEEN.set(false);
+        SQL_CENSUS.merge("adjudicated " + family, 1L, Long::sum);
+    }
+
+    /** The census rows, sorted: {@code adjudicated <family>},
+     * {@code claimed <family>} (a byte verdict was produced),
+     * {@code declined <family> <reason-head>}. */
+    public static java.util.SortedMap<String, Long> sqlCensus() {
+        return new java.util.TreeMap<>(SQL_CENSUS);
+    }
+
+    /** The asserts the database judge decided (its verdict row read; {@link #sqlJudgedInDatabase}). The agreement
+     *  gate's pins read it beside {@link #sqlUnjudgedCount} (the plan path program's step 6, 2026-10-18). */
+    public static long sqlJudgedInDatabaseCount() {
+        return sumOf("judged-in-database ");
+    }
+
+    /** The asserts the database judge named UNJUDGED ({@link #sqlUnjudged}). */
+    public static long sqlUnjudgedCount() {
+        return sumOf("unjudged ");
+    }
+
+    private static long sumOf(String prefix) {
+        long n = 0;
+        for (var e : SQL_CENSUS.entrySet()) {
+            if (e.getKey().startsWith(prefix)) {
+                n += e.getValue();
+            }
+        }
+        return n;
+    }
+
+    /** A rejected fused statement's accounting (the database's rejection, which then fails the body; the planning
+     *  rung's own at {@code VerdictBatch.plan}): counted, with the reason's head and the frames the message names. */
+    public static void fallback(RuntimeException e, java.util.Map<String, String> fragments) {
+        Census.inc(Census.Key.VERDICT_FALLBACKS);
+        String m = String.valueOf(e.getMessage());
+        Census.FALLBACK_REASONS.add(e.getClass().getSimpleName() + ": "
+                + m.substring(0, Math.min(m.length(), 160)).replace('\n', ' ')
+                + attribute(m, fragments));
+    }
+    /** The fragments a database message names (a frame alias appears in DuckDB's binder errors and H2's column
+     *  errors), or an empty string. */
+    static String attribute(String message, java.util.Map<String, String> fragments) {
+        StringBuilder sb = new StringBuilder();
+        for (var e : fragments.entrySet()) {
+            if (e.getKey().startsWith("frame_") && message.contains(e.getKey())) {
+                sb.append(sb.length() == 0 ? " — near " : ", ").append(e.getValue());
+            }
+        }
+        return sb.toString();
+    }
+}

+package com.legend.driver;

+/**
+ * AN APPEAL DECIDED AFTER THE BODY'S RUN, from the plan's record and the run's (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class AppealDecision {
+
+    private AppealDecision() {
+    }
+
+
+    /**
+     * AN APPEAL DECIDED AFTER THE BODY'S RUN, FROM THE PLAN'S DATA (the plan-as-data design's step 8b, 2026-11-08):
+     * {@code a} heard over the texts the run recorded -- a contract's failure, the generator's refusal (S143), or the
+     * rows leg, the edge's ({@code AppealDecider}; step 10). Inside a judging scope that plans nothing.
+     */
+    static TypedSide decideAppeal(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal a, String golden, String ours) {
+        String name = a.name();
+        return switch (a.kind()) {
+            case CONTRACT -> fail(java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(a.contract()) + ": expected " + golden + ", got "
+                    + ours);
+            case TDG -> {
+                // the generator's rows leg planned the generator's transcript and ran it at the verdict -- a run inside
+                // the body's run (THE WALL) and planning at run; the generator is not offered (S143), so the leg is
+                // refused by name (I3, 2026-11-07: its session and the planning it reached deleted)
+                com.legend.base.RunScope.planning("the test-data generator's rows leg");
+                throw new IllegalStateException(name + ": the test-data generator's rows leg is not offered (S143)");
+            }
+            // the rows leg is the edge's (the door hands it to the edge's decider): refused here, by name
+            case ROWS -> throw new IllegalStateException(name + " (sql-text): "
+                    + (a.rows() == null ? "an appeal whose rows were not planned with its assert"
+                            : "an appeal decided with no replay referee"));
+        };
+    }
+

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.driver;
+
+import static com.legend.driver.AssertErrorNative.*;
+
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.HostJudgment;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.SpecCompiler;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCInteger;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCString;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCollection;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedLambda;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedNativeCall;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedSpec;
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.TypedSide;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/**
+ * AN ASSERTERROR JUDGED AT ITS STEP (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class AssertErrorRuns {
+
+    private AssertErrorRuns() {
+    }
+
+    /** THE ARM'S VERDICT at its step (the walker-deletion program, 2026-11-03): the error {@code f} raised -- while it
+     *  was planned ({@code a.caught()}), else reading {@code sides}, f's last statement run -- judged against the expected
+     *  message, line and column with the pure {@code assertError/4} body's exact spellings, the walk's and the program
+     *  road's one rule; a side that raises nothing fails ("No error was thrown"), an error no arm takes (a wall) is
+     *  raised as it was. */
+    static TypedSide judge(HostJudgment.AssertError a, java.util.function.Supplier<List<TypedSide>> sides,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules) {
+        if (a.inner() != null) {
+            // f IS an assert (2026-11-04): its failure is the error assertError matches, as upstream's assertError
+            // catches an assert's failure as any error; the inner assert judged over its sides as any assert, by the
+            // same rules -- a side that raises is that error too
+            String message = null;
+            try {
+                // the inner assert's sides were read with the step's, its caller's message among them: no reader
+                if (HostJudge.judge(a.inner().judgment(), sides, rules, null) == null) {
+                    throw new com.legend.error.NotImplementedException(a.inner().assertion().name()
+                            + " inside assertError: the host judge declined the sides it read");
+                }
+            } catch (com.legend.error.AssertFailed | com.legend.error.DataError | com.legend.error.ModelException e) {
+                message = String.valueOf(e.getMessage());
+            }
+            return judged(a.expected(), a.line(), a.column(), message, null);
+        }
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Failure planned = a.caught();
+        if (planned != null) {
+            // the error f raised while it was planned, as the plan's data: its message; no position (planning sends
+            // no statement, so no database's raise carries one)
+            return judged(a.expected(), a.line(), a.column(), String.valueOf(planned.message()), null);
+        }
+        RuntimeException raised = null;
+        try {
+            sides.get();
+        } catch (com.legend.error.DataError | com.legend.error.ModelException e) {
+            // the errors the arm takes (caught's): any other is a wall and stays loud, raised as it was
+            raised = e;
+        }
+        return judged(a.expected(), a.line(), a.column(), raised == null ? null : String.valueOf(raised.getMessage()),
+                raised instanceof com.legend.error.DataError ? raised.getCause() : null);
+    }
+
+    static TypedSide judged(String expected, @com.legend.base.Nullable Long expectedLine,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable Long expectedColumn, @com.legend.base.Nullable String caughtMessage,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable Throwable caughtCause) {
+        if (caughtMessage == null) {
+            // interpreted AssertError.java: PureAssertFail("No error was
+            // thrown") — a FAIL, not an orchestration error
+            throw new com.legend.error.AssertFailed("No error was thrown");
+        }
+        // B7 (RaisedErrors): messages arrive ALREADY clean — the
+        // Executor funnel unwraps the transport envelope from
+        // platform-raised text (provenance-sentinel scoped); a native
+        // error keeps its class and envelope, and a mismatch against
+        // pure's expectation is then an HONEST failure, never a strip
+        // coincidence. The old broad prefix regex is deleted.
+        String actual = caughtMessage;
+        if (!actual.equals(expected)) {
+            // assertError.pure:24 — the /4 body's assertEquals format,
+            // verbatim
+            throw new com.legend.error.AssertFailed(
+                    "Execution error message mismatch.\nThe actual message"
+                    + " was \"" + actual + "\"\nwhere the expected"
+                    + " message was:\"" + expected + "\"");
+        }
+        // POSITION adjudication (leg 2, assertError.pure:25-26 — message
+        // first, then line, then column, each only when expected): the
+        // raise emission threads the raising call's source span through
+        // the provenance envelope (PureSql.raise -> RaisedErrors
+        // .Positioned). Interpreted pure's matcher receives the raising
+        // expression's source info the same way (AssertError.java:68).
+        // An expectation over an error WITHOUT a captured span is LOUD —
+        // interpreted si.line->toOne() on empty raises too, and a quiet
+        // pass here would launder a native error's missing provenance.
+        if (expectedLine != null || expectedColumn != null) {
+            com.legend.exec.RaisedErrors.Position p = com.legend.exec.RaisedErrors.position(caughtCause);
+            if (p == null) {
+                throw new com.legend.error.AssertFailed(
+                        "assertError line/column: the caught error carries"
+                        + " no source position (a native database error, or"
+                        + " a raise emission without provenance)");
+            }
+            if (expectedLine != null && p.line() != expectedLine) {
+                // assertError.pure:25 — the /4 body's format, verbatim
+                throw new com.legend.error.AssertFailed(
+                        "Execution error line mismatch. Actual: " + p.line()
+                        + " where expected: " + expectedLine);
+            }
+            if (expectedColumn != null && p.column() != expectedColumn) {
+                // assertError.pure:26 — the /4 body's format, verbatim
+                throw new com.legend.error.AssertFailed(
+                        "Execution error column mismatch. Actual: "
+                        + p.column() + " where expected: " + expectedColumn);
+            }
+        }
+        return new TypedSide.Scalar(Boolean.TRUE,
+                "Boolean");
+    }
+
+}

+/**
+ * AN ASSERT JUDGED AT ITS VERDICT STEP, from the plan's facts: the host judgment's run (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class AssertRuns {
+
+    private AssertRuns() {
+    }
+
+    static void familyEntered(String family) {
+        com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlFamily(family);
+    }
+
+    /** The equality arm's census route, as {@link #adjudicate} counts it once the engine-text arm answered. */
+    static void sqlTextRouted(String family) {
+        com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRoute(family, "sql-text");
+    }
+
+    /** The census at a planned assert's turn: its family, counted before any side runs (as {@link #adjudicate}
+     *  counts it). */
+    static void enter(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a) {
+        if (a.family() != null) {
+            familyEntered(a.family());
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The verdict of {@code a}, judged by {@code j} over its sides as the runner read them (their rows, or the error
+     *  reading them raised): a declined judgment counted as the generic path's (null); an engine-text equality's census
+     *  route counted once the arm answered (M10b: never on a failed one, which raises before). */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide judge(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a, HostJudgment j,
+            java.util.function.Supplier<List<TypedSide>> sides,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules,
+            java.util.function.@com.legend.base.Nullable Supplier<TypedSide> message) {
+        TypedSide v = judgedOrFellThrough(HostJudge.judge(j, sides, rules, message));
+        if (v != null && HostJudgment.core(j) instanceof HostJudgment.EngineText e && e.routed()) {
+            sqlTextRouted(a.name());
+        }
+        return v;
+    }
+
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide judgedOrFellThrough(@com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide v) {
+        if (v == null) {
+            com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlFellThrough();
+        }
+        return v;
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * THE PROGRAM ROAD'S VERDICT SEAM (the test-is-the-product plan's M6): {@code a}, planned before the program ran,
+     * judged at its verdict step over the sides the runner read ({@code sides} shapes them, or raises the error
+     * reading them raised: the judgment decides it, {@code HostJudge.judge}) -- counted and reported to
+     * {@code listener} exactly as {@link #tryAdjudicate} counts and reports an assert the walk adjudicates in place:
+     * entered, its verdict reported, a failure (an assert that does not hold, a side's data error) counted as raised
+     * and reported, any other exit counted as raised. A side the database refused is thus counted and reported as an
+     * assert whose side raised inside the arm (M6's audit, S2; one path since 2026-11-03). Null where the judgment
+     * declined the sides it read.
+     */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide judgedAtItsStep(com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.Host h,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener,
+            java.util.function.Supplier<List<TypedSide>> sides, com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules,
+            java.util.function.@com.legend.base.Nullable Supplier<TypedSide> message) {
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a = h.assertion();
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Failure before = a.beforeTheArm();
+        if (before != null) {
+            // raised where the walk raises it, before the arm is entered: no census, no listener (S1)
+            throw com.legend.testexecutionplan.Failures.raised(before);
+        }
+        if (listener == null) {
+            enter(a);
+            return judge(a, h.judgment(), sides, rules, message);
+        }
+        com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlEnter();
+        boolean settled = false;
+        try {
+            enter(a);
+            TypedSide v = judge(a, h.judgment(), sides, rules, message);
+            settled = true;
+            if (v != null) {
+                listener.verdict(a.listenerName(), true, null);
+            }
+            return v;
+        } catch (com.legend.error.AssertFailed | com.legend.error.DataError e) {
+            settled = true;
+            com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRaised();
+            if (e instanceof com.legend.error.AssertFailed af && af.unjudgedReason() != null) {
+                listener.unjudged(a.listenerName(), af.unjudgedReason());
+            }
+            listener.verdict(a.listenerName(), false, e.getMessage());
+            throw e;
+        } finally {
+            if (!settled) {
+                com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRaised();
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** {@code a}'s verdict ROW, the database judge's own statement's (an assertError's, S135, 2026-11-05), judged at
+     *  its step and counted and reported as {@link #judgedAtItsStep} counts and reports a host verdict: entered, its
+     *  verdict to the listener, a failure counted as raised. The row's polarity and message decide. */
+    static void judgedRowAtItsStep(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a, List<Object> row,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener) {
+        if (listener == null) {
+            enter(a);
+            DatabaseRows.verdictOf(a.name(), row);
+            return;
+        }
+        com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlEnter();
+        boolean settled = false;
+        try {
+            enter(a);
+            DatabaseRows.verdictOf(a.name(), row);
+            settled = true;
+            listener.verdict(a.listenerName(), true, null);
+        } catch (com.legend.error.AssertFailed | com.legend.error.DataError e) {
+            settled = true;
+            com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRaised();
+            if (e instanceof com.legend.error.AssertFailed af && af.unjudgedReason() != null) {
+                listener.unjudged(a.listenerName(), af.unjudgedReason());
+            }
+            listener.verdict(a.listenerName(), false, e.getMessage());
+            throw e;
+        } finally {
+            if (!settled) {
+                com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRaised();
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The host half's verdict over its sides HELD for the agreement judge: its verdict, or the failure it raised
+     *  (an assert that does not hold), never null together. */
+    record HostHeld(@com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide verdict, com.legend.error.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertFailed failed) {
+    }
+
+    /**
+     * THE AGREEMENT JUDGE'S HOST HALF AT ITS STEP (the walker-deletion program, 2026-11-02): {@code a} judged over the
+     * sides the runner read, its verdict HELD -- the verdict of record is decided once the database half's rows are in
+     * ({@link #agreedAtItsStep}) -- counted as {@link #tryAdjudicate} counts an assert it adjudicates in place over the
+     * agreement arm (entered; a side's data error counted raised, reported and thrown at once, as the agreement arm
+     * lets it through; any other exit counted raised). A judgment that declined the sides it read is refused by name.
+     */
+    static HostHeld heldAtItsStep(com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.Host h, com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener,
+            java.util.function.Supplier<List<TypedSide>> sides, com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules,
+            java.util.function.@com.legend.base.Nullable Supplier<TypedSide> message) {
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a = h.assertion();
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Failure before = a.beforeTheArm();
+        if (before != null) {
+            throw com.legend.testexecutionplan.Failures.raised(before);
+        }
+        if (listener != null) {
+            com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlEnter();
+        }
+        boolean settled = false;
+        try {
+            enter(a);
+            TypedSide v = null;
+            com.legend.error.AssertFailed failed = null;
+            try {
+                v = judge(a, h.judgment(), sides, rules, message);
+            } catch (com.legend.error.AssertFailed e) {
+                failed = e;
+            }
+            settled = true;
+            if (failed != null) {
+                return new HostHeld(null, failed);
+            }
+            if (v == null) {
+                throw new com.legend.error.NotImplementedException(a.name() + " on the program road: the host"
+                        + " judge declined the sides it read (the walk's generic path has no program form)");
+            }
+            return new HostHeld(v, null);
+        } catch (com.legend.error.DataError e) {
+            settled = true;
+            if (listener != null) {
+                com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRaised();
+                listener.verdict(a.listenerName(), false, e.getMessage());
+            }
+            throw e;
+        } finally {
+            if (!settled && listener != null) {
+                com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlRaised();
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The agreement judge's verdict of record for {@code a} ({@code agreed}: the agreement rule over both halves,
+     *  {@link AgreementArm#agree}), reported to {@code listener} as {@link #tryAdjudicate} reports it: a pass, or a
+     *  failure (an assert that does not hold, a data error) counted raised, its UNJUDGED reason and its verdict told,
+     *  and thrown. */
+    static TypedSide agreedAtItsStep(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener,
+            java.util.function.Supplier<@com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide> agreed) {
+        try {
+            TypedSide v = java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(agreed.get(), "a host verdict the agreement rule dropped");
+            if (listener != null) {
+                listener.verdict(a.listenerName(), true, null);
+            }
+            return v;

+    /** A class VALUE riding a JSON slot inside a struct (a polymorphic field: {@code parameters :
+     *  RelationalOperationElement[*]}) arrives as object or array text -- the field's declared kind says it is a
+     *  structure, so it decodes to the map or list the verdict's key restriction reads (WORLD_MAP §4: the value carries
+     *  its class as {@code __type}); any other value as it is. */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable Object structured(@com.legend.base.Nullable Object v) {
+        if (v instanceof String s) {
+            String t = s.trim();
+            if (t.startsWith("{") || t.startsWith("[")) {
+                return com.legend.sql.Json.parse(t);
+            }
+        }
+        return v;
+    }

+    /** A JSON assert side's ONE document text: a GRAPH result's JSON
+     * IS the {@code String[1]} (a serialize execute's {@code .values}
+     * — leg 2 made its stamp String[1], and the DB-built envelope is
+     * the value); any other result must decode to one string. Null =
+     * not this shape (generic path, loud downstream). The side read already ({@code HostSides}, S2f). */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable String jsonSideTextOf(TypedSide r) {

+            return g.json();
+        }
+        List<Object> v = decodeSide(r);
+        return v.size() == 1 && v.get(0) instanceof String s ? s : null;
+    }

+    static List<Object> decodeSide(
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide r) {
+        List<Object> side = TypedSide.decodeSideValues(r);

+        // cost bracket for V12's fused-verdict design) — measurement
+        // only, fed from the one place every side already flows through
+        com.legend.judge.CanonicalDivergence.v7SideRows(side.size());
+        return side;
+    }

+    static Object one(List<Object> side, String what) {
+        if (side.size() != 1) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(what + " must be one value,"
+                    + " got " + side.size());
+        }
+        return side.get(0);
+    }

+    /** Raw cell flatten — cells arrive ALREADY DECODED (the engine's
+     * value-read conventions ride the FETCH: wrapTdsCanon conforms the
+     * plan in SQL and the executor's label-driven unwrap delivers; the
+     * former host-twin decode, valueRead, is deleted — Java-eviction
+     * close of the disagree-9 burn). */
+    static List<Object> cells(TypedSide.Tabular t) {
+        List<Object> out = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
+        for (com.legend.testexecutionplan.Row r : t.rows()) {
+            out.addAll(r.values());
+        }
+        return out;
+    }

+final class BodyRun implements com.legend.exec.ProgramRunner.ProgramSink, TestRun.Judges {
+    private final TestPlan planned;
+    /** The compatibility edge the run was handed, which records the referee's ledger, or null (lite's rules). */
+    private final com.legend.testexecutionplan.@Nullable Edge edge;
+    private final @Nullable Answer answer;
+    /** The caller's sink, asked for before the program ran (a sent answer), or null (none, or a constant's). */
+    private final @Nullable ResultSink early;
+    /** The database judge's segments, read from the plan (step 5, 2026-11-08), or null under another judge. */
+    private final com.legend.judge.RootLoops.@Nullable Runs databaseRuns;
+    /** A database root's failed row's appeal, by its number in the plan (step 8b, 2026-11-08). */
+    private final java.util.function.IntFunction<com.legend.judge.RootLoops.Appeal> rowAppeals;
+    /** The agreement judge's asserts, read from the plan (step 6, 2026-11-08), or null under another judge. */
+    private final @Nullable AgreementRuns agreementRuns;
+    /** The verdict steps whose engine-text check reads OUR text as a side the step runs, by the origin the walk's
+     *  arm marked for it (M10c item 8). */
+    private final java.util.Map<Integer, com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin> deferredTextOrigins;
+    private final @Nullable AssertListener listener;
+    /** The run's sessions by target, published to the judgments it runs ({@link RunReader#session}). */
+    private final java.util.function.Function<ExecutionPlan.Target, Sessions.Session> sessions;
+    /** The caller's sink holding the walk's contract, once the answer's columns arrive. */
+    private @Nullable ResultSink opened;
+    /** {@code node}'s verdict steps' rules recorded by verdict, each checked against the run's: a planner that
+     *  stamped another run's rules is refused by name, never judged under them. */
+    private void rulesOf(TestPlan.TestStep node, com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules runRules) {
+        switch (node) {
+            case TestPlan.Branch b -> {
+                b.then().forEach(step -> rulesOf(step, runRules));
+                b.otherwise().forEach(step -> rulesOf(step, runRules));
+            }
+            case TestPlan.Verdicts v -> {
+                if (v.rules() != runRules) {
+                    throw new IllegalStateException("a verdict step planned under " + v.rules()
+                            + " in a run judging by " + runRules);
+                }
+                v.verdicts().forEach(each -> rulesOf.put(each.id(), v.rules()));
+            }
+            default -> {
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The rules {@code verdict}'s step names. */
+    private com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules(TestPlan.Verdict verdict) {
+        return java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(rulesOf.get(verdict.id()),
+                () -> "a verdict no step of the plan holds: " + verdict.name());
+    }
+
+    /** The last assert's verdict, once judged: a body ending on an assert answers it. */
+    private @Nullable TypedSide lastVerdict;
+    /** The rules each verdict is judged by, read off the plan's verdict steps (the explicit rule, 2026-11-04): the
+     *  plan is the one carrier; the host judge is handed them at the verdict's step. */
+    private final java.util.Map<Integer, com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules> rulesOf = new java.util.HashMap<>();
+
+    BodyRun(TestPlan planned, com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules runRules,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.@Nullable Edge edge,
+            @Nullable Answer answer, @Nullable ResultSink early, @Nullable AssertListener listener,
+            java.util.function.Function<ExecutionPlan.Target, Sessions.Session> sessions) {
+        this.planned = planned;
+        this.sessions = sessions;
+        this.edge = edge;
+        this.answer = answer;
+        this.early = early;
+        this.listener = listener;
+        // each judge's run read from the plan: its judge, and the segments or pairings its verdict groups carry
+        // (the plan-as-data design's steps 5 and 6, 2026-11-08)
+        TestPlan.Judge judge = planned.judge();
+        this.databaseRuns = judge == TestPlan.Judge.DATABASE
+                ? new com.legend.judge.RootLoops.Runs(planned.steps()) : null;
+        // a database row's appeal heard by its number, the plan's data (step 8b): the texts its row compared recorded
+        java.util.Map<Integer, com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal> appealsPlanned = com.legend.testexecutionplan.PlannedAppeals.byNumber(planned);
+        this.rowAppeals = n -> row -> SqlTextRuns.rowAppealed(com.legend.testexecutionplan.PlannedAppeals.of(appealsPlanned, n), row);
+        this.agreementRuns = judge == TestPlan.Judge.AGREEMENT ? new AgreementRuns(planned.steps(), rowAppeals)
+                : null;
+        planned.steps().forEach(step -> rulesOf(step, runRules));
+        java.util.Map<Integer, com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin> textOrigins = new java.util.HashMap<>();
+        List<TestPlan.TestStep> steps = planned.steps();
+        for (int i = 0; i < steps.size(); i++) {
+            // a verdict group's or a branch's statements under the database judge go under BODY, as the walk's
+            // flush sent them (M7): the plan's judge says so (TestRun.program)
+            if (!(steps.get(i) instanceof TestPlan.Verdicts group)) {
+                continue;
+            }
+            // the host's facts the plan carries on the group's first verdict (step 4, 2026-11-08): an agreement
+            // step's or a host judge's one assert's
+            TestPlan.Host p = judge == TestPlan.Judge.AGREEMENT
+                    || judge == TestPlan.Judge.HOST && group.verdicts().size() == 1
+                    ? group.verdicts().get(0).host() : null;
+            if (p != null) {
+                // an engine-text assert whose text is a side the step runs: sent under the origin the walk's arm
+                // marked for it (M10c item 8)
+                com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin o = AssertRuns.deferredTextOrigin(p.judgment());
+                if (o != null) {
+                    textOrigins.put(i, o);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        this.deferredTextOrigins = textOrigins;
+    }
+
+    TypedSide lastVerdict() {
+        return java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(lastVerdict, "a body ending on an assert judged none");
+    }
+
+    /** The database judge's row for {@code verdict} (M7): to its segment's batch, judged at the segment's last
+     *  verdict by the batch's root loop; a body ending on an assert answers the verdict the walk answers. */
+    @Override
+    public void verdict(TestPlan.Verdict verdict, @Nullable List<Object> row) {
+        if (agreementRuns != null) {
+            // both judges have read once the step's last row is in: the agreement rule decides
+            TypedSide v;
+            {
+                v = agreementRuns.of(verdict).received(verdict, row, listener, reader);
+            }
+            if (v != null) {
+                lastVerdict = v;
+            }
+            return;
+        }
+        com.legend.judge.RootLoops.Runs runs = databaseRuns;
+        if (runs == null) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("a database verdict in a body the host judge planned");
+        }
+        TestPlan.Host hostStep = verdict.host();
+        if (hostStep != null && verdict.wrapper() != null) {
+            // an assertError's verdict the database judges (S135): its row is its statement's, judged alone at its
+            // step -- the host's facts name the assert it reports under
+            {
+                AssertRuns.judgedRowAtItsStep(hostStep.assertion(), java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(row,
+                        "an assertError's verdict statement answered no row"), listener);
+            }
+            lastVerdict = Outcomes.ok();
+            return;
+        }
+        if (hostStep != null) {
+            // an assertError's step under the database judge: judged at its sides (ruling N3) -- or here, at its
+            // turn, where it has no side to read (its error caught at planning, or its inner assert decided there):
+            // the runner hands no sides for an empty base, and until 2026-11-04 such a step was never judged (a
+            // body ending on it failed on its missing verdict, any other passed it silently)
+            if (verdict.base().isEmpty()) {
+                judged(hostStep, List::of, null, rules(verdict));
+            }
+            return;
+        }
+        // a row of a segment: to its root loop, the plan's, judged once the segment's last verdict is in (inside
+        // the program's run: a row the loop planned ahead is read through the program's reader, THE WALL)
+        com.legend.judge.RootLoops.Run run = runs.of(verdict);
+        {
+            run.received(verdict, row);
+            if (runs.closes(verdict)) {
+                run.judge(listener, reader, true, DatabaseRows::verdictOf, rowAppeals);
+            }
+        }
+        lastVerdict = Outcomes.ok();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void fused(TestPlan.Verdicts group, @Nullable DataError rejected) {
+        if (agreementRuns != null) {
+            agreementRuns.of(group.verdicts().get(0)).fused(rejected);
+            return;
+        }
+        com.legend.judge.RootLoops.Runs runs = databaseRuns;
+        if (runs == null) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("a fused verdict group in a body the host judge planned");
+        }
+        runs.of(group.verdicts().get(0)).fused(rejected);
+    }
+
+    /** The assert {@code verdict} planned, judged over the rows the runner read for it -- counted and reported
+     *  as the walk's {@code AssertVerdicts.tryAdjudicate} does: the assert entered, a verdict reported, a
+     *  failure (an assert that does not hold) counted as raised and reported; a side the runner could not read
+     *  comes to {@link #sidesFailed}, judged by the same path. */
+    @Override
+    public void sides(TestPlan.Verdict verdict, List<PlanRunner.Rows> rows) {
+        if (agreementRuns != null) {
+            // the host's verdict held until the database half's rows are in
+            agreementRuns.of(verdict).hostSides(verdict, () -> rows, messageOf(verdict), listener,
+                    rules(verdict));
+            return;
+        }
+        TestPlan.Host p = hostOf(verdict);
+        java.util.function.Supplier<PlanRunner.Rows> read = messageOf(verdict);
+        {
+            judged(p, () -> HostSideRuns.shapedBase(p.assertion().name(), p.shapes(), rows),
+                    read == null ? null : HostSideRuns.message(p.shapes(), read), rules(verdict));
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** The program's reader, while it runs (PlanRunner.ProgramSink#reader). */
+    private com.legend.judge.@Nullable SideReader reader;
+
+    @Override
+    public void reader(com.legend.judge.@Nullable SideReader reader) {
+        this.reader = reader;
+    }
+
+    /** {@code verdict}'s caller's message read on demand (the judge asks only where it fails), or null where the
+     *  verdict carries none. */
+    private java.util.function.@Nullable Supplier<PlanRunner.Rows> messageOf(TestPlan.Verdict verdict) {
+        TestPlan.Side message = verdict.message();
+        if (message == null) {
+            return null;
+        }
+        com.legend.judge.SideReader r = java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(reader,
+                "a verdict's message asked for with no program running");
+        return () -> {
+            try {
+                return r.read(message);
+            } catch (java.io.IOException e) {
+                throw new java.io.UncheckedIOException(e);
+            }
+        };
+    }
+
+    /** A side of {@code verdict}'s that could not be read (the database refused it, or the read raised): judged as
+     *  rows are, the judgment deciding the error (an assertError's answer; any other assert's failure, entered,
+     *  counted and reported as the walk counts and reports an assert whose side raised). */
+    @Override
+    public void sidesFailed(TestPlan.Verdict verdict, RuntimeException failure) {
+        if (agreementRuns != null) {
+            agreementRuns.of(verdict).hostSides(verdict, () -> {
+                throw failure;
+            }, null, listener, rules(verdict));
+            return;
+        }
+        judged(hostOf(verdict), () -> {
+            throw failure;
+        }, null, rules(verdict));
+    }
+
+    /** The host's facts {@code verdict} carries in the plan (step 4, 2026-11-08): the host judge's assert, or,
+     *  under the database judge, an assertError's step (ruling N3: Java compares the error in both judge modes). */
+    private TestPlan.Host hostOf(TestPlan.Verdict verdict) {
+        TestPlan.Host h = verdict.host();
+        if (h == null) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(databaseRuns != null
+                    ? "host sides for a verdict the database judge planned: " + verdict.name()
+                    : "a verdict's sides in a moved body whose plan carries no host facts for it: "
+                            + verdict.name());
+        }
+        return h;
+    }
+
+    private void judged(TestPlan.Host p, java.util.function.Supplier<List<TypedSide>> sides,
+            java.util.function.@Nullable Supplier<TypedSide> message,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules) {
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Assertion a = p.assertion();
+        TypedSide v = AssertRuns.judgedAtItsStep(p, listener, sides, rules, message);
+        if (v == null) {
+            throw new com.legend.error.NotImplementedException(a.name() + " on the program road: the host"
+                    + " judge declined the sides it read (the walk's generic path has no program form)");
+        }
+        lastVerdict = v;
+    }
+
+    /** The origin the verdict step at {@code step} reads our text under: an engine-text verdict step reading our
+     *  text as a side, the origin the arm marked for it (M10c item 8); else null. (A frame let's eager run goes
+     *  under LET and the database judge's steps under BODY by the plan's own facts: TestRun.program.) */
+    com.legend.exec.@Nullable StatementOrigin textOrigin(int step) {
+        return deferredTextOrigins.get(step);
+    }
+
+    /** Whether the program's step {@code step} is the graph's system store's seed step (the planner's). */
+    private boolean systemStore(int step) {
+        return planned.steps().get(step) instanceof TestPlan.Run r
+                && r.node() instanceof ExecutionPlan.Setup u && u.key().equals(ExecutionPlan.Setup.SYSTEM_STORE);
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void columns(List<ExecutionPlan.Column> columns) throws IOException {
+        if (answer != null) {
+            ResultSink asked = early != null ? early : answer.of(planned.answerShape());
+            ResultSink sink = holdingTheWalksContract(asked, columns);
+            opened = sink;
+            sink.columns(columns);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** {@code sink} holding the cardinality the walk holds on a statement's answer (JudgePlans.streamResult): a
+     *  value of at most one row answers no more, a required one at least one, a to-one reader exactly one; a
+     *  constant the walk folds and answers as it is. */
+    private ResultSink holdingTheWalksContract(ResultSink sink, List<ExecutionPlan.Column> columns) {
+        if (!planned.sendsTheAnswer()) {
+            return sink;
+        }
+        return switch (planned.answerShape()) {
+            case VALUE -> {
+                ExecutionPlan.Multiplicity m = columns.get(0).multiplicity();
+                Integer upper = m.upper();
+                yield SideShapes.holding(sink, upper != null && upper <= 1, m.lower(), m.text());
+            }
+            // a relation's toOne() is the database's to check (D2, 2026-11-07: ToOneGuard in the plan's SQL)
+            case RELATION -> sink;
+            // a graph fetch's objects, one per row (the query program's answer since 2026-11-06, the stress
+            // lanes' service queries): the product's streaming graph root, no cardinality the walk held on it
+            case OBJECTS -> sink;
+        };
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void batch(Batch batch) throws IOException {
+        if (opened != null) {
+            opened.batch(batch);
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void end() throws IOException {
+        if (opened != null) {
+            opened.end();
+        }
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void seeded(int step, List<Boolean> answeredRows) {
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Edge e = edge;
+        if (e == null) {
+            return;   // lite's own rules: nothing planned for the edge, no ledger
+        }
+        List<com.legend.testexecutionplan.Extension> texts = planned.extensionsByStep().get(step);
+        if (texts == null && systemStore(step)) {
+            // the graph's system store's seeds are no body's: the walk's ledger recorded none of them
+            return;
+        }
+        if (texts == null) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("seed step " + step + " of a moved body ran " + answeredRows.size()
+                    + " statement(s) with no ledger texts planned at it");
+        }
+        e.seeded(texts, answeredRows);
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void writing(int step) {
+        Appeals.writing();
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public void effectRan(int step) {
+        // the census counts the effect's statements from the plan's own step (its script), whatever the rules
+        if (!(planned.steps().get(step) instanceof TestPlan.Run r
+                && r.node() instanceof ExecutionPlan.Effect effect && effect.script() != null)) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("effect step " + step + " of a moved body is no scripted effect of the"
+                    + " plan");
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < effect.script().statements().size(); i++) {
+            Census.inc(Census.Key.EFFECTS_IN_SCRIPT);
+        }
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Edge e = edge;
+        if (e != null) {
+            List<com.legend.testexecutionplan.Extension> texts = planned.extensionsByStep().get(step);
+            if (texts == null) {
+                throw new IllegalStateException("effect step " + step + " of a moved body ran with no ledger texts"
+                        + " planned at it");
+            }
+            e.effectRan(texts);
+        }
+    }
+}

+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedNativeCall;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedSpec;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedUserCall;
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.TypedSide;
+import com.legend.judge.PureAsserts;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/**
+ * A DATABASE-JUDGED VERDICT ROW READ: holds, or its message raised, or unjudged (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class DatabaseRows {
+
+    private DatabaseRows() {
+    }
+
+    /** The verdict of one row ({@code verdict, expected, actual, unjudged, lenient, holds, message}): unjudged
+     * (counted, the assert fails with the reason), or the row's own answer -- holds, or its message raised (the
+     * test-is-the-product plan's M8b: the polarity and the text are the statement's; this reads two columns). */
+    static TypedSide verdictOf(String name, List<Object> row) {
+        if (com.legend.diagnostics.Diagnostics.dumpSql()) {
+            // the SQL dump's companion: the verdict row the statement returned
+            System.err.println("[verdict] " + name + " verdict=" + row.get(0)
+                    + " expected=" + row.get(1) + " actual=" + row.get(2)
+                    + " unjudged=" + row.get(3) + " holds=" + row.get(5));
+        }
+        Object unjudged = row.get(3);
+        if (unjudged != null) {
+            // the evidence columns ride the message (bounded): an accepted
+            // divergence matches its witness here, and a row is diagnosable
+            // without a re-run
+            return unjudged(name, String.valueOf(unjudged),
+                    "\nexpected: " + Outcomes.excerpt(row.get(1))
+                    + "\nactual:   " + Outcomes.excerpt(row.get(2)));
+        }
+        if (!(row.get(5) instanceof Boolean holds)) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(name + ": the holds column is not a boolean: " + row.get(5));
+        }
+        com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlJudgedInDatabase(name);
+        if (Boolean.TRUE.equals(row.get(4))) {
+            // the declared 2-ULP Float leniency decided it — counted as
+            // host mode counts its own firings
+            com.legend.judge.CanonicalDivergence.sqlUlpPolicy("database " + name);
+            com.legend.diagnostics.Diagnostics.judgeSend("ulp-tolerance", "database " + name);
+        }
+        if (holds) {
+            return Outcomes.ok();
+        }
+        return Outcomes.fail(String.valueOf(row.get(6)));
+    }
+
+
+
+}

+/**
+ * THE HOST JUDGE'S PLANNING: each assert's asks and the judgment it names, with the facts read from the typed tree (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class HostPlans {
+
+    private HostPlans() {
+    }
+
+    // ---- the arm's sides as one verdict step's base (the seam program's S2f, 2026-10-30) ----------------------
+
+    /** A side as {@code AssertVerdicts.side} fetched it: folded where literal, no identity lane. */
+    /** A statement read as a value side: what the walk's generic path plans for an assert no arm plans. */
+    static HostSides.Ask statementAsk(TypedSpec statement, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return valueAsk(statement, hook);
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Ask valueAsk(TypedSpec arg, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Ask(arg, false, true, hook);
+    }
+
+    /** A side on the IDENTITY LANE without a canon rider (F13c: the assert-condition/predicate evaluator; in-SQL
+     *  eq/equal needs instance identity, and the boolean egress keeps every other lane blind to the field): folded
+     *  where literal. */
+    static HostSides.Ask identityAsk(TypedSpec arg,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Ask(arg, true, true, hook);
+    }
+
+    /** A side the canon fetch reads ({@link #sideCanonOf}): never folded, on the identity lane. */
+    static HostSides.Ask canonAsk(TypedSpec arg, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Ask(arg, true, false, hook);
+    }
+
+    /** The static facts of one side, read once at planning. */
+    static HostJudgment.SideFacts facts(TypedSpec s) {
+        return JudgmentFacts.sideFacts(s);
+    }
+
+    /** {@link #tdsEquivalent} planned: the two grids and the tolerances, one verdict's base (M6: the walk read the
+     *  tolerances after the grids, a group of their own; literal tolerances send nothing either way). */
+    static HostSides.Check planTdsEquivalent(List<TypedSpec> targs,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        List<HostSides.Ask> asks = new java.util.ArrayList<>(List.of(valueAsk(targs.get(0), hook),
+                valueAsk(targs.get(1), hook), valueAsk(targs.get(2), hook)));
+        if (targs.size() == 4) {
+            asks.add(valueAsk(targs.get(3), hook));
+        }
+        return new HostSides.Check(asks, new HostJudgment.TdsEquivalent(targs.size() == 4));
+    }
+
+    /** The GRID PAIR ({@code assertEquals} / {@code assertNotEquals} over two statically relation-stamped sides,
+     *  D3): both grids executed in definition order, the grid owner ({@link com.legend.judge.TdsCompare#grids}:
+     *  columns ordered, rows under the order view of the actual side) judges -- never a byte decline. */
+    static HostSides.Check planGridPair(String name, boolean wantEqual, List<TypedSpec> args,
+            List<TypedSpec> letPrefix, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        boolean sorted = com.legend.compiler.spec.OrderView.of(args.get(1), letPrefix)
+                == com.legend.compiler.spec.OrderView.SORTED;
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(0), hook), valueAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.GridPair(name, wantEqual, sorted));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planSize(List<TypedSpec> args, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(1), hook), valueAsk(args.get(0), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.Size(AssertVerdicts.envelopeValuesRead(args.get(0), letPrefix)));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planContains(List<TypedSpec> args,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(0), hook), valueAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.Contains(facts(args.get(0)), facts(args.get(1))));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planTolerance(List<TypedSpec> args,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(0), hook), valueAsk(args.get(1), hook),
+                valueAsk(args.get(2), hook)), new HostJudgment.Tolerance());
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planCondition(String name, TypedSpec cond, boolean wantTrue,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        // forAll-contains SUBSET (the functionvariables idiom
+        // — the harness's audited fc arm, moved to the owner):
+        // both sides evaluate IN THE DATABASE; the membership
+        // fold is assert-level logic judged host-side (a
+        // subquery inside a SQL lambda cannot lower, and
+        // pure's own evaluation of this shape is in-memory).
+        TypedSpec[] fc = AssertVerdicts.forAllContains(cond);
+        if (fc != null) {
+            return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(fc[0], hook), valueAsk(fc[1], hook)),
+                    new HostJudgment.Subset(name, wantTrue));
+        }
+        // F13c: the CONDITION rides the identity lane — eq/
+        // equal over instances compile the engine relation
+        // (identity/key canon); the egress is one boolean, so
+        // no other lane ever sees the identity field
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(identityAsk(cond, hook)), new HostJudgment.Condition(name, wantTrue));
+    }
+
+    /** {@code check} with the caller's failure message (the asserts-as-plan-nodes program, step 2): the message one
+     *  more side, folded where it is a constant (a literal costs no statement). */
+    static HostSides.Check messaged(HostSides.Check check, TypedSpec message,
+            AssertVerdicts.@com.legend.base.Nullable SpliceHook hook) {
+        List<HostSides.Ask> asks = new java.util.ArrayList<>(check.asks());
+        asks.add(new HostSides.Ask(message, false, true, hook));
+        return new HostSides.Check(asks, new HostJudgment.Messaged(check.judgment(), check.asks().size()));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planEmpty(TypedSpec arg, boolean wantEmpty,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        // §8 leg 1: a TABULAR side's emptiness is its ROW count
+        // (engine relation semantics) — no canon involved
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(arg, hook)), new HostJudgment.Empty(wantEmpty));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planEq(List<TypedSpec> args, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix, BodyDriver.ExecEnv env,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        HostJudgment.SideFacts e = facts(args.get(0));
+        HostJudgment.SideFacts a = facts(args.get(1));
+        HostJudgment.Keys keys = JudgmentFacts.keys(args.get(0), args.get(1), env.ctx());
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(canonAsk(args.get(0), hook), canonAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.Eq(e, a, keys));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planInstanceOf(List<TypedSpec> args, BodyDriver.ExecEnv env,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        // the classes the type accepts: itself and its subtree, the model's subtype relation read at planning (the
+        // judgment reads the wire's classifier against the set); a type argument that is no reference names none, and
+        // the judgment declines the side it read (the walk's generic path, as before)
+        String type = AssertVerdicts.typeRefName(args.get(1));
+        java.util.Set<String> accepted = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>();
+        if (type != null) {
+            accepted.add(type);
+            accepted.addAll(env.ctx().subtree(type));
+        }
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(0), hook)), new HostJudgment.InstanceOf(type, accepted));
+    }
+
+    /** {@link #is} planned; null (no side asked) where the pair is not two elements. */
+    static HostSides.@com.legend.base.Nullable Check planIs(String name, List<TypedSpec> args, SpecCompiler specs,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        // ELEMENT IDENTITY (metamodel-as-relations D2/D3): a tracked
+        // element's identity is its row's primary key — `is` over an
+        // element reference and a metamodel row is the same equality
+        // the chain normalizer rewrites to a key compare
+        // (ChainNormalizer.identityEquality); adjudicated as the
+        // condition assert on the identity lane
+        if (AssertVerdicts.elementTyped(args.get(0), specs) && AssertVerdicts.elementTyped(args.get(1), specs)) {
+            TypedSpec cond = com.legend.resolver.ChainNormalizer.identityCondition(
+                    specs.ctx(), args.get(0), args.get(1));
+            return new HostSides.Check(List.of(identityAsk(cond, hook)), new HostJudgment.Is(name));
+        }
+        return null;
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planSameElements(List<TypedSpec> args, boolean gridPair, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix,
+            BodyDriver.ExecEnv env, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        HostJudgment.SideFacts e = facts(args.get(0));
+        HostJudgment.SideFacts a = facts(args.get(1));
+        HostJudgment.Keys keys = JudgmentFacts.keys(args.get(0), args.get(1), env.ctx());
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(canonAsk(args.get(0), hook), canonAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.SameElements(e, a, gridPair, keys));
+    }
+
+    /** The VECTOR judgment planned (M10c item 5): one identity-lane side, the predicate vector; judged by
+     *  {@link #allRowsOf} as {@link #quantified} judges its read. */
+    static HostSides.Check planAllRows(String fqn, TypedSpec predMap, boolean wantTrue, String message,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(identityAsk(predMap, hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.AllRows(fqn, wantTrue, message));
+    }
+
+    /** A quantified assert over rows planned for the program road (HostJudgment.Rows): its asks the zip's arms or the
+     *  one relation source, its arguments read at planning -- a row's field, the row, or a literal; any other argument
+     *  refused by name, never evaluated here (the walk's rule, {@link #rowValue}). Null: another family or source. */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable HostSides.Check planRows(String fqn, TypedSpec source,
+            com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedLambda lam, TypedSpec root, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        com.legend.builtin.NativeFn.Verdict family = com.legend.builtin.NativeFn.Verdict
+                .of(com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.Calls.calleeIdOf(root)).orElse(null);
+        boolean isEquals = family == com.legend.builtin.NativeFn.Verdict.ASSERT_EQUALS;
+        boolean isTolerance = family == com.legend.builtin.NativeFn.Verdict.ASSERT_EQ_WITHIN_TOLERANCE;
+        List<TypedSpec> args = com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.Calls.argsOf(root);
+        if (!isEquals && !isTolerance || args.size() < (isTolerance ? 3 : 2)) {
+            return null;
+        }
+        String binder = lam.parameters().get(0);
+        List<HostJudgment.RowArg> read = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
+        for (TypedSpec arg : args.subList(0, isTolerance ? 3 : 2)) {
+            HostJudgment.RowArg a = rowArg(arg, binder, fqn);
+            if (a == null) {
+                return null;   // an argument computed per element: not this read's
+            }
+            read.add(a);
+        }
+        List<TypedSpec> arms = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
+        TypedSpec bound = com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.Lets.bound(source, letPrefix);
+        zipArms(bound, letPrefix, arms);
+        List<HostSides.Ask> asks = new java.util.ArrayList<>(arms.size());
+        for (TypedSpec arm : arms) {
+            asks.add(valueAsk(arms.size() == 1 ? source : arm, hook));
+        }
+        HostJudgment.Pairing pairing = arms.size() == 1 ? null : pairing(bound, letPrefix, new int[] {0});
+        return new HostSides.Check(asks, new HostJudgment.Rows(fqn, isTolerance, read,
+                com.legend.compiler.element.EqualityKeys.fqnOf(args.get(0).info().type()),
+                com.legend.compiler.element.EqualityKeys.fqnOf(args.get(1).info().type()), pairing));
+    }
+
+    /** A row assert's argument as data: {@link #rowValue}'s shapes, read at planning. */
+    static HostJudgment.@com.legend.base.Nullable RowArg rowArg(TypedSpec arg, String binder, String fqn) {
+        return switch (arg) {
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCast c -> rowArg(c.source(), binder, fqn);
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedVariable v when v.name().equals(binder) ->
+                    new HostJudgment.RowArg.Whole();
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedPropertyAccess pa
+                    when pa.source() instanceof com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedVariable v
+                            && v.name().equals(binder) -> new HostJudgment.RowArg.Field(pa.property());
+            // a literal as its carrier and its text: read back to the same value at the step (the plan-as-data
+            // design's step 4)
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCString l -> new HostJudgment.RowArg.Literal("String", l.value());
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCInteger l -> new HostJudgment.RowArg.Literal(
+                    l.value().getClass().getSimpleName(), l.value().toString());
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCFloat l -> new HostJudgment.RowArg.Literal("Double",
+                    Double.toString(l.value()));
+            case com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedCBoolean l -> new HostJudgment.RowArg.Literal("Boolean",
+                    Boolean.toString(l.value()));
+            // another argument is computed per element: the database computes it (the per-element form's operand
+            // pairs, or the unroll's per-element sides), never this read of the row (2026-11-04)
+            default -> null;
+        };
+    }
+
+    /** A quantified equality element by element on the host (the asserts-as-plan-nodes program, step 5): its one
+     *  side the operand pairs the database computes, each element judged as the element arm judges a pair
+     *  ({@link com.legend.judge.PureAsserts}: the same decision, the same message) -- nothing computed here. */
+    static HostSides.Check planPerElement(String fqn, TypedSpec pairs, com.legend.compiler.element.type.ExprType expected,
+            com.legend.compiler.element.type.ExprType actual, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(pairs, hook)), new HostJudgment.Rows(fqn, false,
+                List.of(new HostJudgment.RowArg.Field("first"), new HostJudgment.RowArg.Field("second")),
+                com.legend.compiler.element.EqualityKeys.fqnOf(expected.type()),
+                com.legend.compiler.element.EqualityKeys.fqnOf(actual.type()), null));
+    }
+
+    /** The zip's pairing over its arms, in {@link #zipArms}' order. */
+    static HostJudgment.Pairing pairing(TypedSpec arm, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix, int[] next) {
+        TypedSpec bound = com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.Lets.bound(arm, letPrefix);
+        if (bound instanceof com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedNativeCall z
+                && com.legend.compiler.element.type.PlatformTypes.COLLECTION_ZIP.equals(z.callee().qualifiedName())
+                && z.args().size() == 2) {
+            HostJudgment.Pairing left = pairing(z.args().get(0), letPrefix, next);
+            HostJudgment.Pairing right = pairing(z.args().get(1), letPrefix, next);
+            return new HostJudgment.Pairing.Zip(left, right);
+        }
+        return new HostJudgment.Pairing.Arm(next[0]++);
+    }
+
+    /** The arms of a (nested) zip, left to right; a source that is no zip is its own one arm. */
+    static void zipArms(TypedSpec source, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix, List<TypedSpec> out) {
+        if (source instanceof com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedNativeCall z
+                && com.legend.compiler.element.type.PlatformTypes.COLLECTION_ZIP.equals(z.callee().qualifiedName())
+                && z.args().size() == 2) {
+            for (TypedSpec arm : z.args()) {
+                TypedSpec bound = com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.Lets.bound(arm, letPrefix);
+                if (bound instanceof com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedNativeCall inner
+                        && com.legend.compiler.element.type.PlatformTypes.COLLECTION_ZIP.equals(
+                                inner.callee().qualifiedName())) {
+                    zipArms(bound, letPrefix, out);
+                } else {
+                    out.add(arm);
+                }
+            }
+            return;
+        }
+        out.add(source);
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planEquals(String name, boolean wantEqual, List<TypedSpec> args, boolean incidental,
+            boolean gridPair, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix, BodyDriver.ExecEnv env, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        HostJudgment.SideFacts e = facts(args.get(0));
+        HostJudgment.SideFacts a = facts(args.get(1));
+        HostJudgment.Keys keys = JudgmentFacts.keys(args.get(0), args.get(1), env.ctx());
+        HostJudgment.FlatFacts flat = new HostJudgment.FlatFacts(
+                AssertVerdicts.bareRowStamp(args.get(0), letPrefix), AssertVerdicts.bareRowStamp(args.get(1), letPrefix),
+                AssertVerdicts.wrappedRelationStamp(args.get(0), letPrefix),
+                AssertVerdicts.wrappedRelationStamp(args.get(1), letPrefix));
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(canonAsk(args.get(0), hook), canonAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.Equals(name, wantEqual, incidental, gridPair, e, a, keys, flat));
+    }
+
+    static HostSides.Check planJsonStringsEqual(List<TypedSpec> args, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook) {
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(0), hook), valueAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.Json(com.legend.compiler.spec.OrderView.of(args.get(1), letPrefix)
+                        == com.legend.compiler.spec.OrderView.INCIDENTAL));
+    }
+
+    /** {@link #rendered} planned; {@code counted} whether the route is counted where the judgment holds (the host
+     *  judge's own plan path counts it, as {@code AssertVerdicts.adjudicate} counts the walk's once the arm answered). */
+    static HostSides.Check planRendered(String name, boolean wantEqual, List<TypedSpec> args, String form,
+            TypedSpec rendered, @com.legend.base.Nullable String eForm, @com.legend.base.Nullable String aForm,
+            boolean orderedForm, List<TypedSpec> letPrefix, @com.legend.base.Nullable AssertVerdicts.SpliceHook hook,
+            boolean counted) {
+        // a BOTH-rendered pair always judges as a multiset (even
+        // sorted queries legally tie-flip between two executions)
+        boolean sorted = orderedForm
+                && (eForm == null ^ aForm == null)
+                && com.legend.compiler.spec.OrderView.of(rendered, letPrefix)
+                        == com.legend.compiler.spec.OrderView.SORTED;
+        return new HostSides.Check(List.of(valueAsk(args.get(0), hook), valueAsk(args.get(1), hook)),
+                new HostJudgment.Rendered(name, wantEqual, form, eForm, aForm, sorted, counted));
+    }
+
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors

+package com.legend.driver;
+
+import static com.legend.driver.HostSides.*;
+
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.HostJudgment;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.SpecCompiler;
+import com.legend.compiler.spec.typed.TypedSpec;
+import com.legend.exec.PlanRunner;
+import com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan;
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan;
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.TypedSide;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+/**
+ * A HOST-JUDGED VERDICT'S SIDES SHAPED AT ITS STEP (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class HostSideRuns {
+
+    private HostSideRuns() {
+    }
+
+    /** The rows the runner read for a verdict's sides, each shaped by the plan's shape at its position (the plan-as-data
+     *  design's step 4, 2026-11-08); the base's alone where the verdict carries a caller's message apart -- the
+     *  rows one fewer than the shapes, the message {@link #message}'s to read. */
+    static List<TypedSide> shapedBase(String name, List<com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape> shapes,
+            List<PlanRunner.Rows> rows) {
+        if (rows.size() != shapes.size() && rows.size() != shapes.size() - 1) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(name + ": the runner handed " + rows.size() + " sides for "
+                    + shapes.size() + " planned");
+        }
+        List<TypedSide> out = new ArrayList<>(rows.size());
+        for (int i = 0; i < rows.size(); i++) {
+            out.add(SideShapes.shape(shapes.get(i), rows.get(i)));
+        }
+        return out;
+    }
+
+    /** The reader of a verdict's caller's message: the last shape's side, read through {@code read} (the program's
+     *  reader) and shaped by the plan -- only where the judgment asks for it, the assert having failed. */
+    static java.util.function.Supplier<TypedSide> message(List<com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape> shapes,
+            java.util.function.Supplier<PlanRunner.Rows> read) {
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape last = shapes.get(shapes.size() - 1);
+        return () -> SideShapes.shape(last, read.get());
+    }
+
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.driver;
+
+/**
+ * THE ORIGIN A TEXT IS READ UNDER, WHILE IT IS PLANNED (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08): the arms mark
+ * what they plan (our rows for the referee, a host side) and the engine-text collector records the mark its deferred
+ * text was planned under, by the NAME of exec's statement origin the run enters at the verdict step. Planning's own
+ * marks: it set and read exec's thread-local before the halves split, and planning reaches no exec.
+ */
+final class OriginMarks {
+
+    private OriginMarks() {
+    }
+
+    /** The innermost mark, by origin name; null where nothing planned is marked. */
+    private static final ThreadLocal<String> CURRENT = new ThreadLocal<>();
+
+    /** A mark entered: its close restores the one before. */
+    interface Scope extends AutoCloseable {
+        @Override
+        void close();
+    }
+
+    /** {@code origin} marked until the scope closes. */
+    static Scope enter(String origin) {
+        String before = CURRENT.get();
+        CURRENT.set(origin);
+        return () -> CURRENT.set(before);
+    }
+
+    /** {@code origin} marked where nothing is, else the mark that is stays. */
+    static Scope enterIfUnmarked(String origin) {
+        return CURRENT.get() == null ? enter(origin) : () -> {
+        };
+    }
+
+    /** The current mark's origin name, or null. */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable String current() {
+        return CURRENT.get();
+    }
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors

+package com.legend.driver;

+import static com.legend.driver.JudgePlans.*;
