+/**
+ * A SIDE'S ROWS SHAPED BY THE PLAN'S SHAPE (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class SideShapes {
+
+    private SideShapes() {
+    }
+
+    /** A side the body driver holds in Java (the doors, door 1, 2026-10-29) written to the caller's sink through a batcher, as a host's rows are: its
+     *  columns as plan columns of the side's Pure types (no slot: the writer spells a cell by its carrier's own kind),
+     *  its rows the side's values; a graph side one row per object of its array (the reader's own reading). */
+    static void host(TypedSide side, com.legend.driver.Answer answer) {
+        com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Multiplicity one = new com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan
+                .Multiplicity(1, 1);
+        com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.TdsResult.Shape shape;
+        java.util.List<com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Column> columns;
+        java.util.List<java.util.List<Object>> rows = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
+        switch (side) {
+            case TypedSide.Scalar s -> {
+                // the driver's Scalar(null) is Pure's [] (an empty optional value): no row, as the plan path answers;
+                // a value is one, [1] as Pure types it (the test-is-the-product plan's §9 item 2; the user,
+                // 2026-11-01: "match upstream engine/pure"): until then [0..1] whatever it held
+                shape = com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.TdsResult.Shape.VALUE;
+                columns = java.util.List.of(hostColumn("value", s.returnType(),
+                        s.value() == null ? new com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Multiplicity(0, 1) : one));
+                if (s.value() != null) {
+                    rows.add(hostRow(s.value()));
+                }
+            }
+            case TypedSide.Collection c -> {
+                shape = com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.TdsResult.Shape.VALUE;
+                columns = java.util.List.of(hostColumn("value", c.returnType(),
+                        new com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Multiplicity(0, null)));
+                for (Object v : c.values()) {
+                    rows.add(hostRow(v));
+                }
+            }
+            case TypedSide.Tabular t -> {
+                shape = com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.TdsResult.Shape.RELATION;
+                java.util.List<com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Column> cs = new java.util.ArrayList<>();
+                for (com.legend.testexecutionplan.Column c : t.columns()) {
+                    // the side's column carries the typer's multiplicity where the judge had one; one otherwise
+                    cs.add(hostColumn(c.name(), c.pureType(), c.multiplicity() == null ? one : c.multiplicity()));
+                }
+                columns = cs;
+                for (com.legend.testexecutionplan.Row r : t.rows()) {
+                    rows.add(new java.util.ArrayList<>(r.values()));
+                }
+            }
+            case TypedSide.Graph g -> {
+                shape = com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.TdsResult.Shape.OBJECTS;
+                columns = java.util.List.of(hostColumn("json", "String", one));
+                com.legend.json.Json.Node parsed = com.legend.json.Json.parse(g.json());
+                if (parsed instanceof com.legend.json.Json.Obj sole) {
+                    // an engine execute()'s values of exactly one object, serialized bare (the engine JsonBuilder's
+                    // cardinality rule, compatibility only: TypedSerializeGraph.engineExecuteCardinality): one object
+                    rows.add(hostRow(com.legend.json.Json.toCompact(sole)));
+                } else if (parsed instanceof com.legend.json.Json.Arr items) {
+                    for (com.legend.json.Json.Node item : items.items()) {
+                        rows.add(hostRow(com.legend.json.Json.toCompact(item)));
+                    }
+                } else {
+                    throw new IllegalStateException("a graph side's JSON is an array of objects or one object; got "
+                            + g.json());
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        try {
+            com.legend.result.Batcher batcher = new com.legend.result.Batcher(columns, answer.of(shape),
+                    com.legend.result.Batcher.DEFAULT_CAPACITY);
+            batcher.start();
+            for (java.util.List<Object> row : rows) {
+                batcher.row(row);
+            }
+            batcher.end();
+        } catch (java.io.IOException e) {
+            throw new java.io.UncheckedIOException(e);
+        }
+    }
+
+    static com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Column hostColumn(String name, String type,
+            com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Multiplicity multiplicity) {
+        return new com.legend.executionplan.ExecutionPlan.Column(name, type, multiplicity, null, null, null);
+    }
+
+    /** A row of one cell, which may be null (a list holding a null, which {@code List.of} refuses). */
+    static java.util.List<Object> hostRow(@com.legend.base.Nullable Object cell) {
+        java.util.List<Object> row = new java.util.ArrayList<>(1);
+        row.add(cell);
+        return row;
+    }
+
+    /** {@code sink} holding the walk's answer contract ({@link Cardinality}): for the body router, whose moved body's
+     *  answer holds the walk's cardinality until the plan carries it (the test-is-the-product plan's §9, D30). */
+    static com.legend.result.ResultSink holding(com.legend.result.ResultSink sink, boolean atMostOne, long atLeast,
+            String multiplicity) {
+        return new Cardinality(sink, atMostOne, atLeast, multiplicity);
+    }
+
+    /** A sink that counts the rows on their way to {@code sink} and refuses, by the collected side's own words, a
+     *  scalar of more than one row and a required root of none. (A relation's toOne() is the database's to check
+     *  since D2, 2026-11-07: ToOneGuard.) */
+    static final class Cardinality implements com.legend.result.ResultSink {
+        private final com.legend.result.ResultSink sink;
+        private final boolean atMostOne;
+        private final long atLeast;
+        private final String multiplicity;
+        private long rows;
+
+        Cardinality(com.legend.result.ResultSink sink, boolean atMostOne, long atLeast, String multiplicity) {
+            this.sink = sink;
+            this.atMostOne = atMostOne;
+            this.atLeast = atLeast;
+            this.multiplicity = multiplicity;
+        }
+
+        @Override
+        public void columns(List<ExecutionPlan.Column> columns) throws java.io.IOException {
+            sink.columns(columns);
+        }
+
+        @Override
+        public void batch(com.legend.result.Batch batch) throws java.io.IOException {
+            rows += batch.size();
+            if (atMostOne && rows > 1) {
+                throw new IllegalStateException("scalar-shaped result returned more than one row — the to-one"
+                        + " contract was not enforced upstream");
+            }
+            sink.batch(batch);
+        }
+
+        @Override
+        public void end() throws java.io.IOException {
+            if (rows == 0 && atLeast >= 1) {
+                throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cast a collection of size 0 to multiplicity " + multiplicity);
+            }
+            sink.end();
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** {@code rows}, read for a side the plan shapes as {@code s}: the legacy executor's shaping rules, read from the
+     *  plan's facts alone (the plan-as-data design's step 4, 2026-11-08; the second copy over the planner's side,
+     *  shape(PlannedSide), deleted by the slice 2 audit, 2026-11-08 -- a planned constant decodes as it did). */
+    static TypedSide shape(com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape s, PlanRunner.Rows rows) {
+        return switch (s.kind()) {
+            case CONSTANT -> {
+                List<Object> values = new ArrayList<>(rows.rows().size());
+                for (List<Object> row : rows.rows()) {
+                    Object cell = row.get(0);
+                    values.add(switch (cell) {
+                        case null -> null;
+                        case String text -> com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.constantValue(text, s.typeName());
+                        default -> throw new IllegalStateException("a constant side's cell is its text, not a "
+                                + cell.getClass().getSimpleName());
+                    });
+                }
+                yield s.scalar() ? new TypedSide.Scalar(values.isEmpty() ? null : values.get(0), s.typeName())
+                        : new TypedSide.Collection(values, s.typeName());
+            }
+            case GRAPH -> new TypedSide.Graph(objectsShape(rows), s.typeName());
+            case TABULAR -> {
+                List<Column> columns = new ArrayList<>(s.columns().size());
+                for (com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.Column c : s.columns()) {
+                    columns.add(new Column(c.name(), c.type(), c.multiplicity()));
+                }
+                if (s.columns().isEmpty()) {
+                    // a raw grid's: the columns its result reported, each typed by the Pure type the wire table named
+                    for (ExecutionPlan.Column c : rows.columns()) {
+                        columns.add(new Column(c.name(), reportedType(c),
+                                new ExecutionPlan.Multiplicity(c.multiplicity().lower() == 0 ? 0 : 1, 1)));
+                    }
+                }
+                yield new TypedSide.Tabular(columns, rowsOf(rows), s.typeName(), s.oneCarrier());
+            }
+            case KEYED -> {
+                List<Column> columns = new ArrayList<>(rows.columns().size());
+                for (ExecutionPlan.Column c : rows.columns()) {
+                    com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.Column fixed = s.columns().stream()
+                            .filter(x -> x.name().equals(c.name())).findFirst().orElse(null);
+                    columns.add(fixed != null ? new Column(fixed.name(), fixed.type(), fixed.multiplicity())
+                            : new Column(com.legend.sql.PivotNames.present(c.name()), pivotColumnType(s, c.name())));
+                }
+                yield new TypedSide.Tabular(columns, rowsOf(rows), s.typeName(), s.oneCarrier());
+            }
+            case VALUES -> valuesShape(valueLane(rows), s);
+        };
+    }
+
+    static List<Row> rowsOf(PlanRunner.Rows rows) {
+        List<Row> out = new ArrayList<>(rows.rows().size());
+        for (List<Object> r : rows.rows()) {
+            out.add(new Row(r));
+        }
+        return out;
+    }
+
+    /** {@link Type.RelationType#pivotColumnType}'s one rule over the plan's columns: a fixed column by its name (the
+     *  presented name's quotes stripped), a per-key one by the template its generated name ends with. */
+    static String pivotColumnType(com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape s, String rawName) {
+        String name = rawName.length() >= 2 && rawName.startsWith("'") && rawName.endsWith("'")
+                && rawName.contains(com.legend.sql.PivotNames.SEPARATOR)
+                ? rawName.substring(1, rawName.length() - 1) : rawName;
+        for (com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.Column c : s.columns()) {
+            if (c.name().equals(name) || c.name().equals(rawName)) {
+                return c.type();
+            }
+        }
+        int sep = name.lastIndexOf(com.legend.sql.PivotNames.SEPARATOR);
+        if (sep >= 0 && !s.templates().isEmpty()) {
+            String template = name.substring(sep + com.legend.sql.PivotNames.SEPARATOR.length());
+            return s.templates().stream().filter(c -> c.name().equals(template)).findFirst()
+                    .map(com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.Column::type)
+                    .orElseThrow(() -> new IllegalStateException("pivot column '" + name + "' matches no aggregate"
+                            + " template " + s.templates().stream()
+                                    .map(com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.Column::name).toList()));
+        }
+        throw new IllegalStateException("the keyed side's column '" + rawName + "' is neither among the schema's"
+                + " columns " + s.columns() + " nor a per-key column of its templates " + s.templates());
+    }
+
+    /** A value side's rules, read from the plan's shape (a required root of no rows refuses; a scalar of two rows
+     *  refuses; a null cell in a collection is the TDSNull value under a TDSNull root, a null slot on the Any and
+     *  variant lanes, a lowering defect otherwise). */
+    static TypedSide valuesShape(PlanRunner.Rows rows, com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape s) {
+        long lower = s.lower();
+        if (s.scalar()) {
+            if (rows.rows().isEmpty() && lower >= 1) {
+                throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cast a collection of size 0 to multiplicity "
+                        + s.multiplicity());
+            }
+            if (rows.rows().size() > 1) {
+                throw new IllegalStateException("scalar-shaped result returned more than one row — the to-one"
+                        + " contract was not enforced upstream");
+            }
+            Object v = rows.rows().isEmpty() ? null : rows.rows().get(0).get(0);
+            if (v instanceof List<?> l && !s.listCarrier()) {
+                return new TypedSide.Collection(new ArrayList<>(l), s.typeName());
+            }
+            return new TypedSide.Scalar(v, s.typeName());
+        }
+        List<Object> values = new ArrayList<>(rows.rows().size());
+        for (List<Object> r : rows.rows()) {
+            Object v = r.get(0);
+            if (v == null) {
+                if (s.tdsNullRoot()) {
+                    values.add(com.legend.sql.SqlExpr.TDS_NULL_CELL);
+                } else if (s.nullableRoot()) {
+                    values.add(null);
+                } else {
+                    throw new IllegalStateException("NULL cell reached COLLECTION egress — the lowerer owns the"
+                            + " null-drop (COMPILER_SHORTCUT_AUDIT §5); a NULL here is a lowering defect, never an"
+                            + " empty");
+                }
+            } else {
+                values.add(v);
+            }
+        }
+        if (rows.rows().isEmpty() && lower >= 1) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("Cannot cast a collection of size 0 to multiplicity " + s.multiplicity());
+        }
+        if (values.size() < lower) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("collection-shaped result holds " + values.size()
+                    + " values, below its declared lower bound " + lower
+                    + " — NULL cells were dropped past the type's own contract");
+        }
+        return new TypedSide.Collection(values, s.typeName());
+    }
+
+    static String objectsShape(PlanRunner.Rows rows) {
+        if (rows.rows().size() != 1) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("a graph fetch side's statement answered " + rows.rows().size()
+                    + " rows: its objects' array is one row");
+        }
+        return switch (rows.rows().get(0).get(0)) {
+            case null -> "";
+            case String objects -> objects;
+            case Object other -> throw new IllegalStateException("a graph fetch side's objects' array is no text: "
+                    + other.getClass().getSimpleName());
+        };
+    }
+    /** A reported column's Pure type, in the plan's spelling: the primitive the wire table named, or Any (its cells
+     *  the driver's kind). */
+    static String reportedType(ExecutionPlan.Column c) {
+        if (com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.PRIMITIVES.contains(c.type())) {
+            return c.type();
+        }
+        if (c.type().equals(com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.ANY)) {
+            return com.legend.testexecutionplan.SideShape.ANY;
+        }
+        throw new IllegalStateException("a reported column's type '" + c.type() + "' is no primitive the wire table"
+                + " names");
+    }
+    /** A value root's rows in the VALUE lane's carrier: a timestamp slot's cell with its digits trimmed
+     *  ({@link com.legend.values.PureDateLiteral#trimmed}) -- the database judge's value-lane canon spells a
+     *  timestamp so ({@code dateTimeText}: a whole second none, {@code .123} three), and so did the JSON rows
+     *  wire's carrier spelling of a value root (step 6), where a grid's cell keeps the nine digits of the engine's
+     *  relational read (its GRID_FETCH canon; step 7, 2026-10-18). A text-carried temporal (a literal's own digits)
+     *  is read as written and stays. */
+    static PlanRunner.Rows valueLane(PlanRunner.Rows rows) {
+        List<ExecutionPlan.Column> columns = rows.columns();
+        boolean any = columns.stream().anyMatch(c -> c.slot() != null && c.slot().startsWith("TIMESTAMP"));
+        if (!any) {
+            return rows;
+        }
+        List<List<Object>> out = new ArrayList<>(rows.rows().size());
+        for (List<Object> r : rows.rows()) {
+            List<Object> cells = new ArrayList<>(r);
+            for (int i = 0; i < columns.size(); i++) {
+                String slot = columns.get(i).slot();
+                if (slot != null && slot.startsWith("TIMESTAMP")
+                        && cells.get(i) instanceof com.legend.values.PureDateLiteral d) {
+                    cells.set(i, d.trimmed());
+                }
+            }
+            out.add(cells);
+        }
+        return new PlanRunner.Rows(columns, out);
+    }
+}

+/**
+ * AN ENGINE-TEXT COMPARISON JUDGED AT ITS STEP, its appeal heard (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: split from its class by side).
+ */
+final class SqlTextRuns {
+
+    private SqlTextRuns() {
+    }
+
+    /** {@code e} judged at its verdict step over the two text sides the runner handed back (the golden a constant of
+     *  the plan, checked against the planned text; ours a constant likewise, or the side the step RAN for a deferred
+     *  text, M10c item 8 -- its one string is our text, and the equality is computed here as the arm computes it): the
+     *  decline reported, then the text decides -- equal holds; else the appeal, the walk's own rows leg, now, with the
+     *  texts in hand. */
+    static TypedSide judge(HostJudgment.EngineText e, List<TypedSide> sides,
+            com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules rules) {
+        // the appeal is heard by its number, the plan's (step 8b): the run records the texts it read, and the edge
+        // decides after the run; the step's rules say whether a failed text is appealed
+        boolean compat = rules == com.legend.testexecutionplan.TestPlan.JudgingRules.COMPILED_PURE_COMPAT;
+        if (sides.size() != 2) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(e.name() + ": the engine-text step handed " + sides.size()
+                    + " sides, two texts expected");
+        }
+        String golden = TypedSide.scalarString(sides.get(0));
+        String ours = TypedSide.scalarString(sides.get(1));
+        if (e.golden() != null && !e.golden().equals(golden) || e.ours() != null && !e.ours().equals(ours)) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(e.name() + ": the verdict step's texts are not the texts the arm planned");
+        }
+        if (e.golden() == null) {
+            // a golden the plan read (2026-11-04, the core program): the two texts compare first, as any equality;
+            // only a failed comparison re-runs the arm over them -- its golden processing and its appeal
+            if (golden == null || ours == null) {
+                return fail(e.name() + " (sql-text): " + (golden == null ? "the golden" : "our text")
+                        + " side, a String to one, read no string at the verdict step");
+            }
+            if (golden.equals(ours)) {
+                if (e.decline() != null) {
+                    declinedNow(e.name(), e.decline());
+                }
+                return ok();
+            }
+            if (!compat) {
+                return EngineTextRules.textsDiffer(e.name(), golden, ours, e.decline());
+            }
+            // compatibility mode: the appeal PLANNED with the assert, over the golden as read (2026-11-06, the plan
+            // ahead: until then the arm was re-run over the step's texts while the program ran, planning as it went).
+            // The arm planned its route for a golden it could not see; a golden whose text would choose another route
+            // -- an engine population statement, a numbered in-list temp -- is refused by name, never re-planned
+            String g = stripChainedPlanWarning(golden);
+            if (g.equals(ours)) {
+                return ok();
+            }
+            if (isPopulationGolden(g) || NAMED_IN_TEMP.matcher(g).find()) {
+                return fail(e.name() + " (sql-text): a golden read at the verdict step whose text chooses a statement"
+                        + " route the plan did not plan (a population or in-list temp statement): " + g);
+            }
+            return Appeals.hold(e.name(), e.appeal(), g, ours, false);
+        }
+        boolean textEqual = e.textEqual();
+        if (e.ours() == null) {
+            if (ours == null) {
+                return fail(e.name() + " (sql-text): our text side, a String to one, read no string at the verdict"
+                        + " step");
+            }
+            textEqual = e.golden().equals(ours);
+        }
+        // the equality arm's census route is the caller's to count once this answered (AssertVerdicts.HostAssert); the
+        // decline reported, the text decides -- equal holds, else the appeal heard, or the texts' failure
+        if (e.decline() != null) {
+            declinedNow(e.name(), e.decline());
+        }
+        String o = java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(ours);
+        return textEqual ? ok() : compat ? Appeals.hold(e.name(), e.appeal(), e.golden(), o, textEqual)
+                : EngineTextRules.textsDiffer(e.name(), e.golden(), o, e.decline());
+    }
+
+
+    /** The ACTUAL text of a failed verdict row ({@code VerdictSql.schema}'s column): our text as the database computed
+     *  it, or null. */
+    static @com.legend.base.Nullable String actualText(List<Object> row) {
+        // by its position, the plan's constant (step 10: the run reaches no lowering)
+        Object actual = row.get(com.legend.testexecutionplan.VerdictRow.ACTUAL);
+        return actual == null ? null : String.valueOf(actual);
+    }
+
+
+
+
+
+    /** The database judge's failed row's appeal heard (step 8b): the texts its row compared, ours read from the row's
+     *  actual cell where the plan reads it, recorded for the decision after the run. */
+    static TypedSide rowAppealed(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal a, List<Object> row) {
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal.Batched b = java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(a.batched(), "a row appeal with no row's texts");
+        if (b.ours() != null) {
+            return Appeals.hold(a.name(), a.number(), b.golden(), b.ours(), b.textEqual());
+        }
+        String read = actualText(row);
+        return read == null
+                ? fail(a.name() + " (sql-text): our text side, a String to one, read no string at the verdict step")
+                : Appeals.hold(a.name(), a.number(), b.golden(), read, b.golden().equals(read));
+    }
+
+    /** A decline reported to the run's listener (the edge's after the run, the body's while it runs). */
+    static void declinedTo(com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener, String name,
+            String reason) {
+        if (listener != null) {
+            listener.declined(name, reason);
+        }
+    }
+
+    /** A decline reported while the body runs, to its listener (the appeals' keeping one). */
+    static void declinedNow(String name, String reason) {
+        declinedTo(Appeals.listenerNow(), name, reason);
+    }
+
+
+
+
+}

diff --git a/core/src/main/java/com/legend/edge/JsonSimple.java b/core/src/main/java/com/legend/edge/JsonSimple.java
new file mode 100644
index 0000000..504563c
--- /dev/null
+++ b/core/src/main/java/com/legend/edge/JsonSimple.java
@@ -0,0 +1,334 @@
+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.edge;
+
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.CompatJson;
+import com.legend.base.Nullable;
+import java.math.BigDecimal;
+import java.math.BigInteger;
+import java.util.ArrayList;
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.LinkedList;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+/**
+ * THE COMPATIBILITY RULES' JSON GRAMMAR (register S144, the user's ruling of 2026-11-07, option 2): json-simple
+ * 1.1.1's lexer and parser state machine ({@code org.json.simple.parser.JSONParser#parse}), ported -- legend-pure's
+ * pin, upstream's {@code equalJsonStrings} grammar. The edge's (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08), found
+ * by the test plan's {@code CompatJson} seam; see {@code JudgeJson} for the grammar's rules as probed.
+ */
+public final class JsonSimple implements CompatJson {
+
+    /** The seam's instance: each read parses with a parser of its own. */
+    public JsonSimple() {
+        this.s = "";
+    }
+
+    @Override
+    public @Nullable Object read(String text) {
+        return new JsonSimple(text).parse();
+    }
+
+    private enum Kind { LEFT_BRACE, RIGHT_BRACE, LEFT_SQUARE, RIGHT_SQUARE, COMMA, COLON, VALUE, EOF }
+
+    private static final int INIT = 0;
+    private static final int FINISHED_VALUE = 1;
+    private static final int IN_OBJECT = 2;
+    private static final int IN_ARRAY = 3;
+    private static final int PASSED_PAIR_KEY = 4;
+    private static final int IN_ERROR = -1;
+
+    private final String s;
+    private int at;
+    private Kind kind = Kind.EOF;
+    private @Nullable Object value;
+
+    JsonSimple(String s) {
+        this.s = s;
+    }
+
+    @Nullable Object parse() {
+        LinkedList<Integer> statuses = new LinkedList<>();
+        LinkedList<@Nullable Object> values = new LinkedList<>();
+        int status = INIT;
+        do {
+            next();
+            switch (status) {
+                case INIT -> {
+                    switch (kind) {
+                        case VALUE -> {
+                            status = FINISHED_VALUE;
+                            statuses.addFirst(status);
+                            values.addFirst(value);
+                        }
+                        case LEFT_BRACE -> {
+                            status = IN_OBJECT;
+                            statuses.addFirst(status);
+                            values.addFirst(new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>());
+                        }
+                        case LEFT_SQUARE -> {
+                            status = IN_ARRAY;
+                            statuses.addFirst(status);
+                            values.addFirst(new ArrayList<Object>());
+                        }
+                        default -> status = IN_ERROR;
+                    }
+                }
+                case FINISHED_VALUE -> {
+                    if (kind == Kind.EOF) {
+                        return values.removeFirst();
+                    }
+                    throw unexpected();
+                }
+                case IN_OBJECT -> {
+                    switch (kind) {
+                        case COMMA -> {
+                        }
+                        case VALUE -> {
+                            if (value instanceof String key) {
+                                values.addFirst(key);
+                                status = PASSED_PAIR_KEY;
+                                statuses.addFirst(status);
+                            } else {
+                                status = IN_ERROR;
+                            }
+                        }
+                        case RIGHT_BRACE -> status = closed(statuses, values);
+                        default -> status = IN_ERROR;
+                    }
+                }
+                case PASSED_PAIR_KEY -> {
+                    switch (kind) {
+                        case COLON -> {
+                        }
+                        case VALUE -> {
+                            statuses.removeFirst();
+                            String key = (String) values.removeFirst();
+                            map(values.getFirst()).put(key, value);
+                            status = statuses.getFirst();
+                        }
+                        case LEFT_SQUARE, LEFT_BRACE -> {
+                            statuses.removeFirst();
+                            String key = (String) values.removeFirst();
+                            Object container = kind == Kind.LEFT_SQUARE ? new ArrayList<Object>()
+                                    : new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+                            map(values.getFirst()).put(key, container);
+                            status = kind == Kind.LEFT_SQUARE ? IN_ARRAY : IN_OBJECT;
+                            statuses.addFirst(status);
+                            values.addFirst(container);
+                        }
+                        default -> status = IN_ERROR;
+                    }
+                }
+                case IN_ARRAY -> {
+                    switch (kind) {
+                        case COMMA -> {
+                        }
+                        case VALUE -> list(values.getFirst()).add(value);
+                        case RIGHT_SQUARE -> status = closed(statuses, values);
+                        case LEFT_SQUARE, LEFT_BRACE -> {
+                            Object container = kind == Kind.LEFT_SQUARE ? new ArrayList<Object>()
+                                    : new LinkedHashMap<String, Object>();
+                            list(values.getFirst()).add(container);
+                            status = kind == Kind.LEFT_SQUARE ? IN_ARRAY : IN_OBJECT;
+                            statuses.addFirst(status);
+                            values.addFirst(container);
+                        }
+                        default -> status = IN_ERROR;
+                    }
+                }
+                default -> throw unexpected();
+            }
+            if (status == IN_ERROR) {
+                throw unexpected();
+            }
+        } while (kind != Kind.EOF);
+        throw unexpected();
+    }
+
+    /** A container closed: the one it sits in resumes, or the root is finished. */
+    private static int closed(LinkedList<Integer> statuses, LinkedList<@Nullable Object> values) {
+        if (values.size() > 1) {
+            statuses.removeFirst();
+            values.removeFirst();
+            return statuses.getFirst();
+        }
+        return FINISHED_VALUE;
+    }
+
+    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
+    private static Map<String, Object> map(@Nullable Object o) {
+        return (Map<String, Object>) java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(o);
+    }
+
+    @SuppressWarnings("unchecked")
+    private static List<Object> list(@Nullable Object o) {
+        return (List<Object>) java.util.Objects.requireNonNull(o);
+    }
+
+    private IllegalStateException unexpected() {
+        return new IllegalStateException("Unexpected token " + kind + " at position " + at
+                + " (json-simple 1.1.1's grammar)");
+    }
+
+    /** The next token: whitespace skipped; an unterminated string at the end is the end. */
+    private void next() {
+        while (at < s.length() && " \t\n\r".indexOf(s.charAt(at)) >= 0) {
+            at++;
+        }
+        value = null;
+        if (at >= s.length()) {
+            kind = Kind.EOF;
+            return;
+        }
+        char c = s.charAt(at);
+        switch (c) {
+            case '{' -> single(Kind.LEFT_BRACE);
+            case '}' -> single(Kind.RIGHT_BRACE);
+            case '[' -> single(Kind.LEFT_SQUARE);
+            case ']' -> single(Kind.RIGHT_SQUARE);
+            case ',' -> single(Kind.COMMA);
+            case ':' -> single(Kind.COLON);
+            case '"' -> string();
+            default -> {
+                if (word("true", Boolean.TRUE) || word("false", Boolean.FALSE) || word("null", null)) {
+                    return;
+                }
+                if (c == '-' || (c >= '0' && c <= '9')) {
+                    number();
+                    return;
+                }
+                throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected character (" + c + ") at position " + at
+                        + " (json-simple 1.1.1's grammar)");
+            }
+        }
+    }
+
+    private void single(Kind k) {
+        at++;
+        kind = k;
+    }
+
+    private boolean word(String w, @Nullable Object v) {
+        if (!s.startsWith(w, at)) {
+            return false;
+        }
+        at += w.length();
+        kind = Kind.VALUE;
+        value = v;
+        return true;
+    }
+
+    private void number() {
+        int start = at;
+        if (s.charAt(at) == '-') {
+            at++;
+        }
+        int digits = digits();
+        if (digits == 0) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("Unexpected character (" + s.charAt(start) + ") at position " + start
+                    + " (json-simple 1.1.1's grammar)");
+        }
+        boolean decimal = false;
+        if (at + 1 < s.length() && s.charAt(at) == '.' && isDigit(s.charAt(at + 1))) {
+            at++;
+            digits();
+            decimal = true;
+        }
+        if (at < s.length() && (s.charAt(at) == 'e' || s.charAt(at) == 'E')) {
+            int mark = at;
+            at++;
+            if (at < s.length() && (s.charAt(at) == '+' || s.charAt(at) == '-')) {
+                at++;
+            }
+            if (digits() == 0) {
+                at = mark;   // no exponent: the number ends before the e, which lexes on its own
+            } else {
+                decimal = true;
+            }
+        }
+        String t = s.substring(start, at);
+        kind = Kind.VALUE;
+        if (decimal) {
+            value = new BigDecimal(t);
+        } else {
+            BigInteger big = new BigInteger(t);
+            if (big.bitLength() >= 64) {
+                // json-simple reads an integer as a Long, and one past it raises (NumberFormatException)
+                throw new IllegalStateException("For input string: \"" + t + "\" (an integer past a Long, refused"
+                        + " by json-simple 1.1.1's grammar)");
+            }
+            value = big.longValue();
+        }
+    }
+
+    private int digits() {
+        int start = at;
+        while (at < s.length() && isDigit(s.charAt(at))) {
+            at++;
+        }
+        return at - start;
+    }
+
+    private static boolean isDigit(char c) {
+        return c >= '0' && c <= '9';
+    }
+
+    /** An ASCII hex digit: json-simple's {@code \\u} takes no other (a fullwidth or Arabic-Indic digit keeps the
+     *  escape's backslash, probed 2026-11-07). */
+    private static boolean isHexDigit(int c) {
+        return c >= '0' && c <= '9' || c >= 'a' && c <= 'f' || c >= 'A' && c <= 'F';
+    }
+
+    /** A string's escapes as json-simple reads them; one unterminated at the end is the end (no token). */
+    private void string() {
+        at++;
+        StringBuilder b = new StringBuilder();
+        while (at < s.length()) {
+            char c = s.charAt(at);
+            if (c == '"') {
+                at++;
+                kind = Kind.VALUE;
+                value = b.toString();
+                return;
+            }
+            if (c != '\\' || at + 1 >= s.length()) {
+                b.append(c);
+                at++;
+                continue;
+            }
+            char e = s.charAt(at + 1);
+            switch (e) {
+                case '"' -> b.append('"');
+                case '\\' -> b.append('\\');
+                case '/' -> b.append('/');
+                case 'b' -> b.append('\b');
+                case 'f' -> b.append('\f');
+                case 'n' -> b.append('\n');
+                case 'r' -> b.append('\r');
+                case 't' -> b.append('\t');
+                case 'u' -> {
+                    if (at + 6 <= s.length() && s.substring(at + 2, at + 6).chars()
+                            .allMatch(JsonSimple::isHexDigit)) {
+                        b.append((char) Integer.parseInt(s.substring(at + 2, at + 6), 16));
+                        at += 6;
+                    } else {
+                        b.append('\\');
+                        at++;
+                    }
+                    continue;
+                }
+                default -> {
+                    // an escape json-simple does not know keeps its backslash; the character reads on its own
+                    b.append('\\');
+                    at++;
+                    continue;
+                }
+            }
+            at += 2;
+        }
+        kind = Kind.EOF;
+    }
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.edge;
+
+import com.legend.testexecutionplan.TypedSide;
+import java.util.List;
+
+/**
+ * THE ROWS LEG OF A FAILED ENGINE TEXT'S APPEAL (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: the edge's, where the
+ * test road decided it): the stores' fixtures provided by the harness, our rows read through the run (each a plan of
+ * its own on the body's sessions), the golden replayed by the referee over the model, its verdict the appeal's.
+ */
+final class RowsLeg {
+
+    private RowsLeg() {
+    }
+
+    /** The rows leg itself, from the plan's data: OUR rows as planned, the golden replayed via the referee, judged. */
+    static TypedSide rowsLegNow(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal a, String golden, String ours, boolean textEqual,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable SqlReplayReferee referee, com.legend.compiler.element.ModelContext ctx,
+            com.legend.driver.AppealRun run, com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener) {
+        String name = a.name();
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal.Rows r = a.rows();
+        if (r == null) {
+            // no fallback (2026-11-06, the plan ahead): an appeal's rows and stores are planned with its assert
+            throw new IllegalStateException(name + " (sql-text): an appeal whose rows were not planned with its assert");
+        }
+        if (referee == null) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException(name + " (sql-text): an appeal decided with no replay referee");
+        }
+        // the stores' fixtures, provided by the harness before the rows are read (asked by name: it planned them ahead)
+        if (listener != null) {
+            for (String store : r.stores()) {
+                java.util.function.BooleanSupplier f = listener.fixtureAhead(store);
+                if (f != null) {
+                    f.getAsBoolean();
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal.Unlisted unlisted = r.rows().unlisted();
+        if (unlisted != null) {
+            // our rows' planning failed with a class off the closed list: the same decline, in its words
+            return underivable(listener, name, unlisted.simpleName(), unlisted.message(), textEqual, golden, ours);
+        }
+        TypedSide rows;
+        boolean priorSuspend = com.legend.judge.SqlTypeCensus.probeSuspended();
+        try {
+            com.legend.judge.SqlTypeCensus.probeSuspend(true);
+            try (var __o = com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin.enter(com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin.REFEREE_OURS)) {
+                rows = run.read(r.rows());
+            }
+        } catch (com.legend.base.RunScope.Refused e) {
+            // the runtime wall's refusal is never a rows-underivable decline: it fails the body by name
+            throw e;
+        } catch (RuntimeException e) {
+            // the rows leg is underivable -- counted, text stays the contract (§3.7: a counted decline, visible, never
+            // silent; DataError joined RuntimeException at the seam)
+            return underivable(listener, name, e.getClass().getSimpleName(), e.getMessage(), textEqual, golden, ours);
+        } finally {
+            com.legend.judge.SqlTypeCensus.probeSuspend(priorSuspend);
+        }
+        SqlReplayReferee.ReplayFacts facts = new SqlReplayReferee.ReplayFacts(r.extentSubset(), r.ordered(),
+                r.sortKeys());
+        if (r.population().side() != null || r.population().failure() != null
+                || r.population().unlisted() != null) {
+            if (rows != null) {
+                // the PAGE-MEMBERSHIP verdict's population (batch 0.5b): our rows for the same chain without its tail
+                // page. No catch: the paged read just executed, so a failure here is a FAULT of ours and fails the
+                // test loudly -- never a decline that rescues the text
+                com.legend.judge.SqlTypeCensus.probeSuspend(true);
+                try (var __o = com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin.enter(com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin.REFEREE_OURS)) {
+                    TypedSide population = run.read(r.population());
+                    if (population != null) {
+                        facts = facts.withPopulation(population);
+                    }
+                } finally {
+                    com.legend.judge.SqlTypeCensus.probeSuspend(priorSuspend);
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        if (rows == null) {
+            declinedTo(listener, name, "rows-underivable");
+            return textEqual ? com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.ok()
+                    : com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.fail(name + " (sql-text, rows underivable):"
+                            + " expected " + golden + ", got " + ours);
+        }
+        List<SqlReplayReferee.TempTable> temps = r.temps().stream()
+                .map(t -> new SqlReplayReferee.TempTable(t.name(), t.kind(), t.values())).toList();
+        com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal.InListSeed seed = r.seed();
+        SqlReplayReferee.RowVerdict rv;
+        try (var __o = com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin.enter(com.legend.exec.StatementOrigin.REFEREE_GOLDEN)) {
+            rv = r.goldenPlan() != null
+                ? referee.verifyPlan(run.session(r.on()), r.goldenPlan(), r.planBindings(),
+                        rows, r.mappingFqn(), r.classFqn(), facts, ctx)
+                : referee.verify(run.session(r.on()),
+                        r.replaySql() != null ? r.replaySql() : golden,
+                        rows, r.mappingFqn(), r.classFqn(), facts, ctx,
+                        temps.isEmpty() ? (seed == null ? List.of()
+                                : inListTemps(seed, golden)) : temps);
+        }
+        if (listener != null) {
+            listener.refereed(name, rv.outcome().name());
+        }
+        return switch (rv.outcome()) {
+            case MATCH -> {
+                // rows are the verdict (§0); text is a census number
+                yield com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.ok();
+            }
+            case DIVERGED -> com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.fail(name + " (sql-text ROW verdict —"
+                    + " golden rows vs ours diverged, whatever the text"
+                    + " said): " + rv.detail());
+            case FAULT -> com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.fail(name + " (sql-text: referee FAULT — the"
+                    + " referee's own machinery failed, the text cannot"
+                    + " stand in): " + rv.detail());
+            case DECLINED -> {
+                // referee could not answer: text is the contract,
+                // decline counted (§3.7)
+                declinedTo(listener, name, "oracle-declined");
+                yield textEqual ? com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.ok()
+                        : com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.fail(name + " (sql-text, oracle declined: "
+                                + rv.detail() + "): expected " + golden
+                                + ", got " + ours);
+            }
+        };
+    }
+    /** Our rows underivable: the decline counted, the text the contract (§3.7), the failure in its words. */
+    static TypedSide underivable(com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener, String name,
+            String failedAs, @com.legend.base.Nullable String message, boolean textEqual, String golden, String ours) {
+        declinedTo(listener, name, "rows-underivable");
+        return textEqual ? com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.ok()
+                : com.legend.testexecutionplan.Outcomes.fail(name + " (sql-text, rows underivable — " + failedAs + ": " + message + "): expected " + golden
+                        + ", got " + ours);
+    }
+    /** The temp table the golden reads for the planned in-list seed: its one numbered {@code tempTableForIn_N}, or
+     *  none. */
+    static List<SqlReplayReferee.TempTable> inListTemps(com.legend.testexecutionplan.Appeal.InListSeed seed,
+            String golden) {
+        var m = com.legend.testexecutionplan.EngineTextRules.NUMBERED_IN_TEMP.matcher(golden);
+        java.util.Set<String> names = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>();
+        while (m.find()) {
+            names.add(m.group());
+        }
+        return names.size() != 1 ? List.of()
+                : List.of(new SqlReplayReferee.TempTable(names.iterator().next(), seed.kind(), seed.values()));
+    }
+
+    private static void declinedTo(com.legend.testexecutionplan.@com.legend.base.Nullable AssertListener listener,
+            String name, String reason) {
+        if (listener != null) {
+            listener.declined(name, reason);
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0

+package com.legend.sql;
+
+/**
+ * A DYNAMIC PIVOT'S GENERATED COLUMN NAMES (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08, moved from the compiler's
+ * {@code Type.RelationType}, which delegates here): a per-key column is named {@code <key>__|__<template>}, presented in
+ * its quotes as the engine labels it. Here, below every reader, so the run shapes a keyed side without the compiler.
+ */
+public final class PivotNames {
+
+    private PivotNames() {
+    }
+
+    /** The separator between a pivot key's value and its aggregate template's name. */
+    public static final String SEPARATOR = "__|__";
+
+    /** {@code physical} as the engine presents it: a generated name in its quotes, any other as it is. */
+    public static String present(String physical) {
+        return physical.contains(SEPARATOR) && !(physical.startsWith("'") && physical.endsWith("'"))
+                ? "'" + physical + "'"
+                : physical;
+    }
+}

+package com.legend.testexecutionplan;

+/**
+ * THE ENGINE'S GOLDEN SQL TEXTS, READ (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: one home for what the
+ * engine-text asserts' planning and their run both read of a golden): the chained plan's warning line, the population
+ * statement's shape, and the in-list temp tables a golden names.
+ */
+public final class EngineTextRules {
+
+    private EngineTextRules() {
+    }
+
+    /** The engine's index-less {@code sqlRemoveFormatting($res)} over a CHAINED plan prints statement 0 and appends
+     *  this warning line (relationalMappingExecution.pure): the referee reads the SQL and drops the message before
+     *  replay -- a spec-text shape, like the population statement's. */
+    public static final String CHAINED_PLAN_WARNING = "\nWarning: Results only shown for first relational query.";
+
+    /** {@code golden} without the chained plan's warning line. */
+    public static String stripChainedPlanWarning(String golden) {
+        int at = golden.indexOf(CHAINED_PLAN_WARNING);
+        return at < 0 ? golden : golden.substring(0, at);
+    }
+
+    /** The engine's population statement shape (batch 67): the ONE recognizer of that golden text. */
+    public static boolean isPopulationGolden(String golden) {
+        return golden.toLowerCase(java.util.Locale.ROOT).startsWith("select distinct");
+    }
+
+    /** An in-list temp table a golden names by its parameter. */
+    public static final java.util.regex.Pattern NAMED_IN_TEMP =
+            java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("tempTableForIn_([A-Za-z_][A-Za-z0-9_]*)");
+
+    /** An in-list temp table a golden names by its number. */
+    public static final java.util.regex.Pattern NUMBERED_IN_TEMP =
+            java.util.regex.Pattern.compile("tempTableForIn_(\\d+)");
+
+    /** A text that differs from its golden, in clean mode: the assert's failure, the first difference named. */
+    public static TypedSide textsDiffer(String name, String golden, String ours,
+            @com.legend.base.Nullable String decline) {
+        return Outcomes.fail(name + " (sql-text): our text differs from the golden" + (decline != null ? " (" + decline + ")"
+                : "") + ": " + Outcomes.firstTextDiff(golden, ours));
+    }
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.testexecutionplan;
+
+import com.legend.base.Nullable;
+
+/**
+ * THE TWO TEXTS OF {@code assertJsonStringsEqual} as both judges read them (register S144, the user's ruling of
+ * 2026-11-07, option 2): lite's own runs read strict JSON -- a text that is not JSON is refused; the compatibility
+ * rules read them as upstream does. Upstream's {@code equalJsonStrings} ({@code CompiledSupport.jsonStringEquals})
+ * parses both with json-simple 1.1.1, legend-pure's pin, whose grammar this is, probed against the 1.1.1 jar
+ * (GATES 2026-11-07): commas and colons are skipped wherever they stand, so a missing or extra one reads; an
+ * unterminated string at the very end reads as the end; any other token after the root value is refused; an escape
+ * json-simple does not know keeps its backslash; {@code true}, {@code false} and {@code null} are lowercase; a number
+ * is {@code -?[0-9]+(\.[0-9]+)?([eE][-+]?[0-9]+)?}, an integer past a Long refused; whitespace is space, tab, CR
+ * and LF alone; a {@code \\u} escape takes four ASCII hex digits. Values are lite's (a Long, a BigDecimal for a
+ * fraction or an exponent), as {@code com.legend.sql.Json} reads them: the comparison is the judges' own.
+ * Measured on the corpus (2026-11-07, both databases): two goldens read only under the compatibility grammar, the
+ * graph-fetch milestoning goldens ending {@code ]"}. The compatibility grammar is the edge's, through {@link CompatJson} (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08).
+ */
+public final class JudgeJson {
+
+    private JudgeJson() {
+    }
+
+    /** {@code text} read under {@code rules}; a text the rules refuse raises {@link IllegalStateException}. */
+    public static @Nullable Object read(String text, TestPlan.JudgingRules rules) {
+        if (rules == TestPlan.JudgingRules.COMPILED_PURE_COMPAT) {
+            CompatJson grammar = Grammar.ONE;
+            if (grammar == null) {
+                throw new IllegalStateException("the compatibility rules' JSON grammar is the edge's (json-simple 1.1.1):"
+                        + " no CompatJson is registered on this classpath");
+            }
+            return grammar.read(text);
+        }
+        try {
+            com.legend.json.Json.parse(text);
+        } catch (IllegalArgumentException notJson) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("not JSON: " + notJson.getMessage(), notJson);
+        }
+        return com.legend.sql.Json.parse(text);
+    }
+
+    /** The registered compatibility grammar, found once: the edge's, or none. */
+    private static final class Grammar {
+        static final @Nullable CompatJson ONE = load();
+
+        private static @Nullable CompatJson load() {
+            for (CompatJson c : java.util.ServiceLoader.load(CompatJson.class, CompatJson.class.getClassLoader())) {
+                return c;
+            }
+            return null;
+        }
+    }
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.testexecutionplan;
+
+/**
+ * A VERDICT'S OUTCOMES, spelled once for the asserts' planning and their run alike (the plan-as-data design's step 10,
+ * 2026-11-08: each half held its own copy): a held assert's answer, a failure in the platform's vocabulary, and the
+ * database judge's unjudged outcome, counted.
+ */
+public final class Outcomes {
+
+    private Outcomes() {
+    }
+
+    /** A held assert's answer. */
+    public static TypedSide ok() {
+        return new TypedSide.Scalar(Boolean.TRUE, "Boolean");
+    }
+
+    /** The assert's failure: verdicts speak the platform vocabulary. */
+    public static TypedSide fail(String message) {
+        throw new com.legend.error.AssertFailed(message);
+    }
+
+    /** THE ONE UNJUDGED OUTCOME (leg 3.3, audit §4y): the database judge declined the shape -- counted, then raised as
+     *  a verdict failure that CARRIES its reason (the runner's ledger reads the type, never the text). */
+    public static TypedSide unjudged(String name, String why) {
+        return unjudged(name, why, "");
+    }
+
+    /** {@link #unjudged(String, String)}, {@code detail} riding the message only. */
+    public static TypedSide unjudged(String name, String why, String detail) {
+        com.legend.diagnostics.VerdictCensus.sqlUnjudged(name, why);
+        throw com.legend.error.AssertFailed.unjudged(why, name + ": UNJUDGED in database mode — " + why + detail);
+    }
+
+    /** A verdict row's evidence column for a message: at most 600
+     * characters (a grid text drowns the diagnosis past that). */
+    public static String excerpt(@com.legend.base.Nullable Object evidence) {
+        String s = String.valueOf(evidence);
+        return s.length() <= 600 ? s : s.substring(0, 600) + "…(" + s.length() + " chars)";
+    }
+    /** One-line first-difference sketch of two rendered texts (failure
+     * message position — the full texts drown the diagnosis). */
+    public static String firstTextDiff(String e, String a) {
+        String[] el = e.split("\n", -1);
+        String[] al = a.split("\n", -1);
+        if (el.length != al.length) {
+            return "line-count " + el.length + " != " + al.length
+                    + " (expected first line: " + el[0] + ")";
+        }
+        for (int i = 0; i < el.length; i++) {
+            if (!el[i].equals(al[i])) {
+                return "line " + i + ": expected <" + el[i] + "> got <"
+                        + al[i] + ">";
+            }
+        }
+        return "texts differ only in leniency-adjudicated cells";
+    }
+}

+// Copyright 2026 Legend Contributors
+// SPDX-License-Identifier: Apache-2.0
+
+package com.legend.testexecutionplan;
+
+import java.util.LinkedHashMap;
+import java.util.List;
+import java.util.Map;
+
+/**
+ * THE APPEALS A TEST PLAN CARRIES, READ (the plan-as-data design's step 10, 2026-11-08: the run's reads of the plan,
+ * moved from the test planner's {@code AppealsByVerdict}): by number, the stores they name, one by its number.
+ */
+public final class PlannedAppeals {
+
+    private PlannedAppeals() {
+    }
+
+    /** The stores whose fixtures {@code plan}'s appeals ask the harness to provide before the run (step 8c: the plan
+     *  names them; until then the harness's registry was filled while the asserts were planned). */
+    public static java.util.Set<String> stores(TestPlan plan) {
+        java.util.Set<String> out = new java.util.LinkedHashSet<>();
+        for (Appeal a : byNumber(plan).values()) {
+            Appeal.Rows r = a.rows();
+            if (r != null) {
+                out.addAll(r.stores());
+            }
+        }
+        return out;
+    }
+    /** The appeals {@code plan} carries, by number, in their numbers' order -- the order their arms were planned, so
+     *  the stores they name are provided in that order (the slice 2 audit, 2026-11-08: the harness's provision never
+     *  overlays, so its order is the fixtures' order; by verdict id it was the plan's walk's). */
+    public static Map<Integer, Appeal> byNumber(TestPlan plan) {
+        Map<Integer, Appeal> found = new java.util.HashMap<>();
+        int last = 0;
+        for (List<Extension> at : plan.extensionsByVerdict().values()) {
+            for (Extension e : at) {
+                if (e instanceof Appeal a) {
+                    found.put(a.number(), a);
+                    last = Math.max(last, a.number());
+                }
+            }
+        }
+        Map<Integer, Appeal> out = new LinkedHashMap<>();
+        for (int n = 1; n <= last; n++) {
+            Appeal a = found.get(n);
+            if (a != null) {
+                out.put(n, a);
+            }
+        }
+        return out;
+    }
+    /** The appeal numbered {@code n} the plan carries: one heard that the plan does not carry is refused by name. */
+    public static Appeal of(Map<Integer, Appeal> planned, int n) {
+        Appeal a = planned.get(n);
+        if (a == null) {
+            throw new IllegalStateException("an appeal heard (number " + n + ") that the test plan does not carry");
+        }
+        return a;
+    }
+}

+     *  planning (its check against the compiler's value) and at the run (the plan-as-data design's step 10,
+     *  2026-11-08: one rule below both halves). */
+    public static Object constantValue(String s, String type) {
+        return switch (type) {
+            case "String" -> s;
+            case "Boolean" -> switch (s) {
+                case "true" -> Boolean.TRUE;
+                case "false" -> Boolean.FALSE;
+                default -> throw new IllegalStateException("a Boolean constant's cell '" + s + "'");
+            };
+            case "Integer" -> {
+                java.math.BigInteger b = new java.math.BigInteger(s);
+                yield b.bitLength() < 64 ? (Object) b.longValue() : b;
+            }
+            default -> throw new com.legend.error.NotImplementedException("a constant side of type " + type
+                    + ": the folds admit String, Integer and Boolean");
+        };
+    }

+            @com.legend.base.Nullable TypedSide r) {

+                }
+                yield out;
+            }

+            // the owner) — parsed structures; the lattice/JsonCompare

+                    "assert verdict over a " + r.getClass().getSimpleName()
+                    + " side — grid asserts stay with their own"
+                    + " compare owners");
+        };
+    }

+/** The run reads a verdict row's columns by the test plan's positions ({@link VerdictRow}); the lowering's statement
+ *  answers them in {@link VerdictSql#schema}'s order: the two pinned together (the plan-as-data design's step 10). */
+class VerdictRowOrderTest {
+
+    @Test
+    void theRunsPositionsAreTheStatementsColumns() {
+        List<String> names = VerdictSql.schema().columns().stream().map(c -> c.name()).toList();
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.VERDICT, names.get(VerdictRow.VERDICT));
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.EXPECTED, names.get(VerdictRow.EXPECTED));
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.ACTUAL, names.get(VerdictRow.ACTUAL));
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.UNJUDGED, names.get(VerdictRow.UNJUDGED));
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.LENIENT, names.get(VerdictRow.LENIENT));
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.HOLDS, names.get(VerdictRow.HOLDS));
+        assertEquals(VerdictSql.MESSAGE, names.get(VerdictRow.MESSAGE));
+        assertEquals(7, names.size());
+    }
+}
